    59	const NEVER_SERVE = [
    60	  /ledger/i, /review-raw/i, /reviews?\//i, /packets?\//i, /\.git/i,
    61	  /\.env/i, /credential/i, /secret/i, /token/i, /\.key$/i, /\.pem$/i,
    62	];
    63	
    64	// The cookie value is an HMAC of the token, not the token. If the cookie jar
    65	// leaks, it yields a value that is useless against any other run, and the
    66	// operator's actual token is not in it.
    67	function sessionFor(token) {
    68	  return crypto.createHmac('sha256', 'aegis-dashboard-session').update(String(token)).digest('base64url');
    69	}
    70	
    71	function timingSafeEqual(a, b) {
    72	  const ab = Buffer.from(String(a));
    73	  const bb = Buffer.from(String(b));
    74	  // Compare a fixed-length digest so length itself is not a side channel.
    75	  const ah = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(ab).digest();
    76	  const bh = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bb).digest();
    77	  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(ah, bh);
    78	}
    79	
    80	function parseArgs(argv) {
    81	  const a = { port: 8791, host: '127.0.0.1' };
    82	  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    83	    const t = argv[i];
    84	    if (t === '--port') a.port = Number(argv[++i]);
    85	    else if (t === '--host') a.host = argv[++i];
    86	    else if (t === '--token') a.token = argv[++i];
    87	    else if (t === '--allow-non-loopback') a.allowNonLoopback = true;
    88	    else if (t === '--i-understand-the-exposure') a.acknowledged = true;
    89	    else if (t === '--print-config') a.printConfig = true;
    90	  }
    91	  return a;
    92	}
    93	
    94	const LOOPBACK = new Set(['127.0.0.1', 'localhost', '::1']);
    95	
    96	/**
    97	 * Validate configuration. Returns {ok, reason, config}. Pure, so the refusal
    98	 * rules are testable without opening a socket.
    99	 */
   100	function validateConfig(args, env = process.env) {
   101	  if (!Number.isInteger(args.port) || args.port < 1 || args.port > 65535) {
   102	    return { ok: false, code: 'BAD_PORT', reason: `invalid port ${args.port}` };
   103	  }
   104	
   105	  const loopback = LOOPBACK.has(args.host);
   106	  if (!loopback) {
   107	    if (!args.allowNonLoopback) {
   108	      return {
   109	        ok: false, code: 'NON_LOOPBACK_REFUSED',
   110	        reason: `refusing to bind ${args.host}. This serves internal engineering state; binding beyond loopback requires --allow-non-loopback.`,
   111	      };
   112	    }
   113	    if (!args.acknowledged) {
   114	      return {
   115	        ok: false, code: 'EXPOSURE_UNACKNOWLEDGED',
   116	        reason: 'binding beyond loopback additionally requires --i-understand-the-exposure. Two flags, because a temporary open port is rarely temporary.',
   117	      };
   118	    }
   119	  }
   120	
   121	  // Auth is mandatory everywhere, including loopback.
   122	  let token = args.token || env.AEGIS_DASHBOARD_TOKEN || null;
   123	  let generated = false;
   124	  if (!token) {
   125	    if (!loopback) {
   126	      return {
   127	        ok: false, code: 'TOKEN_REQUIRED',
   128	        reason: 'a non-loopback bind requires an explicit --token or AEGIS_DASHBOARD_TOKEN. A generated token printed to a terminal is not an access-control plan for an exposed port.',
   129	      };
   130	    }
   131	    token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
   132	    generated = true;
   133	  }
   134	  if (token.length < 24) {
   135	    return { ok: false, code: 'WEAK_TOKEN', reason: `token is ${token.length} characters; minimum 24.` };
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  return { ok: true, config: { ...args, host: args.host, token, generated, loopback } };
   139	}
   140	
   141	function isNeverServe(p) {
   142	  return NEVER_SERVE.some((re) => re.test(p));
   143	}
   144	
   145	function handler(config) {
   146	  return (req, res) => {
   147	    const started = Date.now();
   148	    const url = new URL(req.url, `http://${config.host}:${config.port}`);
   149	    const pathname = url.pathname;
   150	
   151	    const deny = (status, msg) => {
   152	      res.writeHead(status, {
   153	        'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8',
   154	        'cache-control': 'no-store',
   155	        'x-content-type-options': 'nosniff',
   156	        'referrer-policy': 'no-referrer',
   157	      });
   158	      res.end(msg + '\n');
   159	      log(req, status, started);
   160	    };
   161	
   162	    if (req.method !== 'GET' && req.method !== 'HEAD') {
   163	      return deny(405, 'read-only host: only GET and HEAD are served');
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    // Auth first. Nothing below this line runs for an unauthenticated caller.
   167	    //
   168	    // THREE ACCEPTED CREDENTIALS, and the third is load-bearing:
   169	    // a token in the URL authenticates the DOCUMENT but not its SUBRESOURCES.
   170	    // The browser fetches state.js with no Authorization header and no query
   171	    // string, so a header-or-query-only design 401s the script tag and renders
   172	    // a blank dashboard — which is worse than an error, because the page looks
   173	    // like it loaded. On a valid token we set a derived, HttpOnly session
   174	    // cookie so subresources authenticate too. The cookie carries an HMAC of
   175	    // the token rather than the token itself, so the browser's cookie jar
   176	    // never holds the credential the operator actually typed.
   177	    const bearer = (req.headers.authorization || '').replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, '');
   178	    const queryToken = url.searchParams.get('token') || '';
   179	    const cookies = Object.fromEntries(
   180	      String(req.headers.cookie || '').split(';').map((c) => {
   181	        const i = c.indexOf('=');
   182	        return i === -1 ? [c.trim(), ''] : [c.slice(0, i).trim(), c.slice(i + 1).trim()];
   183	      }).filter(([k]) => k)
   184	    );
   185	    const sessionValue = sessionFor(config.token);
   186	    const primary = bearer || queryToken;
   187	    const authed =
   188	      (primary && timingSafeEqual(primary, config.token)) ||
   189	      (cookies.aegis_session && timingSafeEqual(cookies.aegis_session, sessionValue));
   190	
   191	    if (!authed) {
   192	      res.writeHead(401, {
   193	        'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8',
   194	        'www-authenticate': 'Bearer realm="AEGIS"',
   195	        'cache-control': 'no-store',
   196	      });
   197	      res.end('unauthorized\n');
   198	      log(req, 401, started);
   199	      return;
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    // Establish the session cookie whenever a primary credential was used, so
   203	    // the document's subresources can authenticate on the next request.
   204	    const setCookie = (primary && timingSafeEqual(primary, config.token))
   205	      ? [`aegis_session=${sessionValue}; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/; Max-Age=3600`]
   206	      : null;
   207	
   208	    if (isNeverServe(pathname)) return deny(403, 'refused: this class of path is never servable');
   209	
   210	    const entry = SERVABLE[pathname];
   211	    if (!entry) return deny(404, 'not found (only the dashboard projection is served here)');
   212	
   213	    // Resolve inside the dashboard directory and verify containment. The
   214	    // allow-list already prevents traversal; this catches a future mistake in
   215	    // the allow-list itself.
   216	    const file = path.resolve(DASHBOARD, entry.file);
   217	    if (!file.startsWith(DASHBOARD + path.sep)) return deny(403, 'refused: path escapes the dashboard directory');
   218	    if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
   219	      return deny(503, `UNAVAILABLE: ${entry.file} has not been generated. Run:\n  node builder-control/aegis-state.cjs --out builder-control/dashboard/state.js`);
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    const body = fs.readFileSync(file);
   223	    res.writeHead(200, {
   224	      ...(setCookie ? { 'set-cookie': setCookie } : {}),
   225	      'content-type': entry.type,
   226	      'content-length': body.length,
   227	      'cache-control': 'no-store',
   228	      'x-content-type-options': 'nosniff',
   229	      'referrer-policy': 'no-referrer',
   230	      'x-frame-options': 'DENY',
   231	      // The dashboard is self-contained: inline styles, no external anything.
   232	      'content-security-policy':
   233	        "default-src 'none'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data:; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'",
   234	    });
   235	    if (req.method === 'HEAD') res.end(); else res.end(body);
   236	    log(req, 200, started);
   237	  };
   238	}
   239	
   240	// Log the path and status, never the token or query string.
   241	function log(req, status, started) {
   242	  const p = String(req.url || '').split('?')[0];
   243	  process.stdout.write(`[aegis-host] ${status} ${req.method} ${p} ${Date.now() - started}ms\n`);
   244	}
   245	
   246	function start(args) {
   247	  const v = validateConfig(args);
   248	  if (!v.ok) {
   249	    process.stderr.write(`\nAEGIS HOSTING REFUSED\n  rule  : ${v.code}\n  reason: ${v.reason}\n\nNothing is listening.\n`);
   250	    return { ok: false, code: v.code };
   251	  }
   252	  const config = v.config;
   253	  const server = http.createServer(handler(config));
   254	  server.listen(config.port, config.host, () => {
   255	    process.stdout.write('\nAEGIS DASHBOARD — LOCAL HOST\n');
   256	    process.stdout.write('='.repeat(56) + '\n');
   257	    process.stdout.write(`bound   : http://${config.host}:${config.port}  ${config.loopback ? '(loopback only)' : '*** EXPOSED BEYOND LOOPBACK ***'}\n`);
   258	    process.stdout.write(`auth    : required${config.generated ? ' (token generated for this run)' : ' (token supplied)'}\n`);
   259	    process.stdout.write(`serves  : ${Object.keys(SERVABLE).join(', ')} — nothing else\n`);
   260	    // A SUPPLIED token is never echoed. The operator already has it, and this
   261	    // banner routinely lands in a log file, a scrollback buffer, or a CI
   262	    // artifact — which would put a live credential at rest in all three.
   263	    // A GENERATED token has to be shown once or the run is unusable, and it
   264	    // dies with the process.
   265	    if (config.generated) {
   266	      process.stdout.write(`open    : http://${config.host}:${config.port}/?token=${config.token}\n`);
   267	      process.stdout.write('          ^ generated for this run only; it is not stored and dies with this process.\n');
   268	    } else {
   269	      process.stdout.write(`open    : http://${config.host}:${config.port}/?token=<the token you supplied>\n`);
   270	      process.stdout.write('          (a supplied token is deliberately never echoed — this banner gets logged)\n');
   271	    }
   272	    process.stdout.write('\nThe repository, ledger, packets, review records and raw reviewer\n');
   273	    process.stdout.write('transcripts are NOT reachable from this process.\n');
   274	  });
   275	  return { ok: true, server, config };
   276	}
   277	
   278	if (require.main === module) {
   279	  const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
   280	  if (args.printConfig) {
   281	    const v = validateConfig(args);
   282	    console.log(JSON.stringify(v.ok ? { ok: true, host: v.config.host, port: v.config.port, loopback: v.config.loopback, authRequired: true, servable: Object.keys(SERVABLE) } : v, null, 2));
   283	    process.exit(v.ok ? 0 : EXIT_REFUSED);
   284	  }
   285	  const r = start(args);
   286	  if (!r.ok) process.exit(EXIT_REFUSED);
   287	}
   288	
   289	module.exports = { validateConfig, handler, start, sessionFor, SERVABLE, NEVER_SERVE, isNeverServe, LOOPBACK };
     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	/**
     3	 * aegis-state.cjs — the ONLY thing the dashboard is allowed to read.
     4	 *
     5	 * This is a projector, not a source. It reads real artifacts, types them, and
     6	 * emits a snapshot in which every rendered fact carries the path it came from.
     7	 * It computes nothing it cannot cite.
     8	 *
     9	 * THE THREE HONEST ABSENCES
    10	 * A dashboard lies in three ways, and each has a required rendering here:
    11	 *   UNAVAILABLE  the source does not exist or could not be read
    12	 *   STALE        the source exists but its observation is older than the
    13	 *                declared threshold — with the age shown, not hidden
    14	 *   UNVERIFIED   nobody checked; there is no evidence either way
    15	 * None of these is ever upgraded to a healthy value. There is no default that
    16	 * means "probably fine": an unprobed connector is UNKNOWN, and a missing
    17	 * reviewer record is UNVERIFIED.
    18	 *
    19	 * THE AUTHORITY BOUNDARY, MECHANICALLY
    20	 * A connector whose `plane` is not "INTEGRATION" is REFUSED, not warned about.
    21	 * Engineering authority lives in the control plane; a registry entry claiming
    22	 * otherwise is a configuration attack surface, not a preference.
    23	 *
    24	 * FAILURE ISOLATION
    25	 * engineering{} and integration{} are separate objects and are never blended.
    26	 * A failed connector cannot downgrade verified engineering, and a failed sync
    27	 * is never rendered as a success.
    28	 *
    29	 *   node builder-control/aegis-state.cjs [--json] [--subject-sha <sha>] [--packet <p>]
    30	 *
    31	 * Exit: 0 snapshot produced (its contents may be full of failures — that is a
    32	 *       successful projection of a failing system) · 2 usage · 3 refused
    33	 */
    34	'use strict';
    35	
    36	const fs = require('fs');
    37	const path = require('path');
    38	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    39	
    40	const HERE = __dirname;
    41	const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..');
    42	const REGISTRY = path.join(HERE, 'connector-registry.json');
    43	const CANON = path.join(HERE, 'TOOL-CAPABILITY-CANON.json');
    44	const LEDGER = path.join(HERE, 'ledger.json');
    45	const ENGOS = path.join(HERE, 'engineering-os.cjs');
    46	
    47	const EXIT_PASS = 0;
    48	const EXIT_USAGE = 2;
    49	const EXIT_REFUSED = 3;
    50	
    51	const rel = (p) => path.relative(ROOT, p);
    52	
    53	// The declared workflow sequence. Drift is measured against this and nothing
    54	// else, so "what was required" is a fact in one place rather than an opinion
    55	// held in several.
    56	const REQUIRED_STAGES = [
    57	  { id: 'spec', label: 'Spec pinned' },
    58	  { id: 'build', label: 'Build' },
    59	  { id: 'deterministic', label: 'Deterministic checks' },
    60	  { id: 'codex', label: 'Independent review' },
    61	  { id: 'copilot', label: 'Repository guardian' },
    62	  { id: 'grok', label: 'Adversarial red team' },
    63	  { id: 'gate', label: 'Binding gate' },
    64	  { id: 'runtime', label: 'Runtime validation' },
    65	  { id: 'checkpoint', label: 'Checkpoint' },
    66	];
    67	
    68	function readJSON(p) {
    69	  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
    70	}
    71	
    72	// Every value the UI renders goes through one of these two constructors, so a
    73	// field physically cannot reach the screen without a provenance path or an
    74	// explicit absence.
    75	function value(v, source, extra = {}) {
    76	  return { value: v, source: rel(source), ...extra };
    77	}
    78	function absent(kind, reason, source) {
    79	  return { value: null, state: kind, reason, source: source ? rel(source) : null };
    80	}
    81	
    82	function ageMinutes(iso, now) {
    83	  const t = Date.parse(iso);
    84	  if (Number.isNaN(t)) return null;
    85	  return Math.floor((now - t) / 60000);
    86	}
    87	
    88	// ── connectors ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    89	function projectConnectors(now) {
    90	  if (!fs.existsSync(REGISTRY)) {
    91	    return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `connector registry not found at ${rel(REGISTRY)}`, connectors: [] };
    92	  }
    93	  let reg;
    94	  try { reg = readJSON(REGISTRY); }
    95	  catch (e) { return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `connector registry unreadable: ${e.message}`, connectors: [] }; }
    96	
    97	  const threshold = Number(reg.stalenessThresholdMinutes) > 0 ? Number(reg.stalenessThresholdMinutes) : 60;
    98	  const vocab = new Set(reg.healthVocabulary || []);
    99	  const connectors = [];
   100	
   101	  for (const c of reg.connectors || []) {
   102	    // Refuse, do not warn. A connector claiming control-plane standing is the
   103	    // one thing this whole amendment exists to make impossible.
   104	    if (c.plane !== 'INTEGRATION') {
   105	      const err = new Error(
   106	        `connector "${c.connectorId}" declares plane "${c.plane}". Only INTEGRATION-plane connectors may be loaded. ` +
   107	        'External systems execute actions; they never hold engineering authority.'
   108	      );
   109	      err.refused = true;
   110	      throw err;
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    // An unprobed connector is UNKNOWN. Never HEALTHY by omission.
   114	    let health = vocab.has(c.health) ? c.health : 'UNKNOWN';
   115	    const ev = c.healthEvidence || null;
   116	    let staleness = null;
   117	    if (!ev || !ev.observedAt) {
   118	      health = 'UNKNOWN';
   119	      staleness = { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no dated health evidence has ever been recorded' };
   120	    } else {
   121	      const age = ageMinutes(ev.observedAt, now);
   122	      if (age === null) {
   123	        health = 'UNKNOWN';
   124	        staleness = { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: `unparseable observedAt "${ev.observedAt}"` };
   125	      } else if (age > threshold) {
   126	        // Staleness does NOT overwrite the last known health — it qualifies it.
   127	        // Overwriting would destroy the only information we have; hiding it
   128	        // would let an hours-old reading pose as live.
   129	        staleness = { state: 'STALE', ageMinutes: age, thresholdMinutes: threshold,
   130	          reason: `last observed ${age} minute(s) ago, threshold ${threshold}` };
   131	      } else {
   132	        staleness = { state: 'FRESH', ageMinutes: age };
   133	      }
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    connectors.push({
   137	      connectorId: c.connectorId,
   138	      label: c.label || c.connectorId,
   139	      provider: c.provider || 'UNKNOWN',
   140	      plane: c.plane,
   141	      executionPath: c.executionPath || 'UNKNOWN',
   142	      legacy: c.status === 'LEGACY_OPTIONAL',
   143	      health,
   144	      staleness,
   145	      authStatus: c.authStatus || 'UNKNOWN',
   146	      capabilities: c.capabilities || [],
   147	      declaredNotSupported: c.declaredNotSupported || [],
   148	      failureCount: typeof c.failureCount === 'number' ? c.failureCount : null,
   149	      lastSuccess: c.lastSuccess || null,
   150	      lastFailure: c.lastFailure || null,
   151	      riskLevel: c.riskLevel || 'UNKNOWN',
   152	      evidence: ev ? { observedAt: ev.observedAt, method: ev.method, result: ev.result } : null,
   153	      authorityNote: c.authorityNote || null,
   154	      source: rel(REGISTRY),
   155	    });
   156	  }
   157	  return { state: 'OK', thresholdMinutes: threshold, connectors };
   158	}
   159	
   160	// ── reviewers (from the tool canon — availability with dated evidence) ──────
   161	function projectReviewers(now) {
   162	  if (!fs.existsSync(CANON)) {
   163	    return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `tool canon not found at ${rel(CANON)}`, reviewers: [] };
   164	  }
   165	  let canon;
   166	  try { canon = readJSON(CANON); }
   167	  catch (e) { return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `tool canon unreadable: ${e.message}`, reviewers: [] }; }
   168	
   169	  const wanted = { 'codex-local': 'independent review', 'grok-cli': 'adversarial red team' };
   170	  const reviewers = [];
   171	  for (const [toolId, role] of Object.entries(wanted)) {
   172	    const t = (canon.tools || []).find((x) => x.toolId === toolId);
   173	    if (!t) {
   174	      reviewers.push({ toolId, role, availability: 'UNAVAILABLE',
   175	        reason: 'not present in the Tool Capability Canon', evidence: null, source: rel(CANON) });
   176	      continue;
   177	    }
   178	    const ev = t.availabilityEvidence || null;
   179	    reviewers.push({
   180	      toolId, role,
   181	      label: t.label || toolId,
   182	      availability: t.availability || 'UNKNOWN',
   183	      enabled: t.enabled !== false,
   184	      evidence: ev ? { observedAt: ev.observedAt, method: ev.method, result: ev.result } : null,
   185	      evidenceAgeMinutes: ev && ev.observedAt ? ageMinutes(ev.observedAt, now) : null,
   186	      source: rel(CANON),
   187	    });
   188	  }
   189	  return { state: 'OK', reviewers };
   190	}
   191	
   192	// ── engineering state (the gate's own verdict — never re-judged here) ───────
   193	function projectEngineering(args) {
   194	  const a = [ENGOS, '--gate-done', '--json'];
   195	  if (args.base) a.push('--base', args.base);
   196	  if (args.head) a.push('--head', args.head);
   197	  if (args.diffLines) a.push('--diff-lines', String(args.diffLines));
   198	  for (const c of args.changed || []) a.push('--changed', c);
   199	  if (args.packet) a.push('--packet', args.packet);
   200	  if (args.subjectSha) a.push('--subject-sha', args.subjectSha);
   201	  const r = spawnSync('node', a, { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
   202	
   203	  // A BLOCKED gate exits 3. That is a successful reading of a blocked system,
   204	  // not a failure to read — so it is projected, not swallowed.
   205	  let parsed = null;
   206	  try { parsed = JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { /* handled below */ }
   207	  if (!parsed) {
   208	    return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE',
   209	      reason: `could not read a gate verdict (exit ${r.status}): ${(r.stderr || '').trim().split('\n')[0] || 'no output'}`,
   210	      source: rel(ENGOS) };
   211	  }
   212	
   213	  const stageEvidence = deriveStages(parsed);
   214	  return {
   215	    state: 'OK',
   216	    gateExit: r.status,
   217	    verdict: parsed.ok ? parsed.state : 'BLOCKED',
   218	    lane: parsed.classification ? parsed.classification.lane : 'UNKNOWN',
   219	    highRisk: parsed.classification ? !!parsed.classification.highRisk : null,
   220	    requiredReviewers: parsed.classification ? parsed.classification.requiredReviewers : [],
   221	    subjectSha256: parsed.subject ? parsed.subject.subjectSha256 : null,
   222	    subjectPaths: parsed.subject ? parsed.subject.subjectPaths : [],
   223	    excludedAsEvidence: parsed.subject ? parsed.subject.excludedAsEvidence : [],
   224	    problems: parsed.problems || [],
   225	    observed: parsed.observed || [],
   226	    unverified: parsed.unverified || [],
   227	    stages: stageEvidence,
   228	    source: rel(ENGOS),
   229	  };
   230	}
   231	
   232	// Map the gate verdict onto the declared stage sequence. This does not decide
   233	// anything — it re-expresses the gate's own problems as stage states so the
   234	// drift is drawable. A stage with no positive evidence is UNVERIFIED, which is
   235	// distinct from FAILED: nobody looked, versus somebody looked and it failed.
   236	function deriveStages(parsed) {
   237	  const problems = parsed.problems || [];
   238	  const observed = (parsed.observed || []).join(' | ');
   239	  const has = (re) => problems.some((p) => re.test(p.rule + ' ' + p.detail));
   240	
   241	  const stageFor = (id) => {
   242	    switch (id) {
   243	      case 'spec':
   244	        if (has(/ENGOS-SPEC-UNPINNED/)) return { state: 'FAILED', reason: 'a source of truth is neither pinned nor marked UNVERIFIED' };
   245	        if (/spec pin:/.test(observed)) return { state: 'PASS', reason: observed.match(/spec pin: [^|]*/)[0].trim() };
   246	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no spec pin was evaluated' };
   247	      case 'build':
   248	        // CONFIRMED FINDING (codex REV-…-codex, CRITICAL/HIGH set, #8): this
   249	        // returned PASS unconditionally. A diff existing is not evidence that
   250	        // it builds — nothing in a gate verdict speaks to compilation at all.
   251	        // Rendering PASS here was the projector fabricating a positive state,
   252	        // which is precisely what the amendment forbids.
   253	        if (!parsed.subject || !(parsed.subject.subjectPaths || []).length) {
   254	          return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: 'no subject paths — there is nothing here to build' };
   255	        }
   256	        if (/deterministic checks executed here: (\d+)\/\1 passed/.test(observed)) {
   257	          return { state: 'PASS', reason: 'the gate executed the declared checks and they passed' };
   258	        }
   259	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'a subject diff exists, but nothing in this verdict verifies it builds' };
   260	      case 'deterministic':
   261	        if (has(/ENGOS-DETERMINISTIC-FAILED/)) return { state: 'FAILED', reason: 'a declared check failed' };
   262	        if (/deterministic checks executed here/.test(observed)) return { state: 'PASS', reason: 'checks executed by the gate' };
   263	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'the gate did not run the checks (no --run-checks); they may have passed elsewhere, but this is not evidence of it' };
   264	      case 'codex':
   265	      case 'grok': {
   266	        const req = (parsed.classification && parsed.classification.requiredReviewers) || [];
   267	        if (!req.includes(id)) return { state: 'NOT_REQUIRED', reason: `not required for lane ${parsed.classification ? parsed.classification.lane : '?'}` };
   268	        if (has(new RegExp(`ENGOS-REVIEWER-UNAVAILABLE.*${id}|${id}.*UNAVAILABLE`, 'i'))) return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: 'the reviewer could not run' };
   269	        if (has(new RegExp(`ENGOS-REVIEW-REJECTED.*${id}`, 'i'))) return { state: 'FAILED', reason: 'the reviewer returned REJECT' };
   270	        if (has(new RegExp(`ENGOS-REVIEW-MISSING.*\\b${id}\\b`, 'i'))) return { state: 'MISSING', reason: 'required, and no review record is bound to this subject' };
   271	        // CONFIRMED FINDING (#8): this used to fall through to PASS after
   272	        // checking only three failure shapes. Any rule the checks above did not
   273	        // recognise — a stale binding, short coverage, ambiguous records, a
   274	        // wrong packet — silently rendered as an approval. Absence of a known
   275	        // failure is not evidence of a pass. Require the gate to have positively
   276	        // observed this reviewer's disposition instead.
   277	        if (new RegExp(`(^|\\|)\\s*${id}: (APPROVE|APPROVE_WITH_NOTES)`, 'm').test(observed)) {
   278	          return { state: 'PASS', reason: 'the gate observed an approval bound to this subject' };
   279	        }
   280	        if (problems.some((pr) => new RegExp(id, 'i').test(pr.rule + ' ' + pr.detail))) {
   281	          const hit = problems.find((pr) => new RegExp(id, 'i').test(pr.rule + ' ' + pr.detail));
   282	          return { state: 'FAILED', reason: hit.rule + ': ' + String(hit.detail).slice(0, 120) };
   283	        }
   284	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no approval was observed for this reviewer on this subject' };
   285	      }
   286	      case 'copilot':
   287	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'advisory reviewer; entitlement unproven and no record collected' };
   288	      case 'gate':
   289	        return parsed.ok
   290	          ? { state: 'PASS', reason: parsed.state }
   291	          : { state: 'BLOCKED', reason: `${problems.length} unmet rule(s)` };
   292	      case 'runtime':
   293	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'runtime validation is a separate step and has not run' };
   294	      case 'checkpoint':
   295	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no checkpoint is claimed by this gate verdict' };
   296	      default:
   297	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'unmapped stage' };
   298	    }
   299	  };
   300	
   301	  return REQUIRED_STAGES.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, label: s.label, ...stageFor(s.id) }));
   302	}
   303	
   304	// ── recent events (the ledger IS the event bus — no second store) ───────────
   305	function projectEvents(limit) {
   306	  if (!fs.existsSync(LEDGER)) {
   307	    return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `ledger not found at ${rel(LEDGER)}`, events: [] };
   308	  }
   309	  let l;
   310	  try { l = readJSON(LEDGER); }
   311	  catch (e) { return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `ledger unreadable: ${e.message}`, events: [] }; }
   312	  if (!Array.isArray(l)) return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: 'ledger is not an array', events: [] };
   313	  const events = l.slice(-limit).reverse().map((e) => ({
   314	    entryId: e.entryId, ts: e.ts, gate: e.gate, status: e.status,
   315	    agentId: e.agentId, packetId: e.packetId || null,
   316	    blockRule: e.blockRule || null,
   317	    operation: e.operation || null,
   318	  }));
   319	  return { state: 'OK', total: l.length, events, source: rel(LEDGER) };
   320	}
   321	
   322	// ── snapshot ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   323	function snapshot(args) {
   324	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
   325	  const now = Date.parse(nowIso);
   326	  const engineering = projectEngineering(args);
   327	  const integration = { connectors: projectConnectors(now) };
   328	
   329	  // Failure isolation, made structural: these are two objects. There is no
   330	  // combined "overall health" field, because the moment one exists somebody
   331	  // will let a failed connector darken a verified build.
   332	  return {
   333	    generatedAt: nowIso,
   334	    contract: {
   335	      absences: ['UNAVAILABLE', 'STALE', 'UNVERIFIED'],
   336	      note: 'Engineering and integration state are separate and are never blended. No field in this snapshot is synthesised; every value cites the artifact it came from.',
   337	    },
   338	    engineering,
   339	    integration,
   340	    // A THIRD plane, deliberately not merged into either of the others. It is
   341	    // not engineering (it holds no authority) and not integration (a Notion
   342	    // disagreement is not a connector fault). Separating it is precisely what
   343	    // makes "engineering VERIFIED / knowledge CONFLICT" a renderable pair
   344	    // rather than a contradiction something has to resolve.
   345	    knowledge: (() => {
   346	      try { return require('./knowledge-mirror.cjs').project({ now }); }
   347	      catch (e) {
   348	        // A refusal is a real, reportable state — never silently omitted.
   349	        return { state: 'REFUSED', reason: e.message, records: [], conflicts: 0 };
   350	      }
   351	    })(),
   352	    reviewers: projectReviewers(now),
   353	    events: projectEvents(Number(args.events) > 0 ? Number(args.events) : 12),
   354	  };
   355	}
   356	
   357	// ── CLI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   358	function parseArgs(argv) {
   359	  const a = {};
   360	  // CONFIRMED FINDING #8: the projector only accepted --packet and
   361	  // --subject-sha, so gateDone recomputed from `git diff HEAD` — empty once the
   362	  // reviewed change is committed. The dashboard then showed an empty subject
   363	  // for work that had actually been reviewed. The exact range the gate used is
   364	  // now forwarded rather than reconstructed from the working tree.
   365	  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
   366	    const t = argv[i];
   367	    if (t === '--subject-sha') a.subjectSha = argv[++i];
   368	    else if (t === '--packet') a.packet = argv[++i];
   369	    else if (t === '--events') a.events = argv[++i];
   370	    else if (t === '--json') a.json = true;
   371	    else if (t === '--out') a.out = argv[++i];
   372	  }
   373	  return a;
   374	}
   375	
   376	if (require.main === module) {
   377	  const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
   378	  let snap;
   379	  try {
   380	    snap = snapshot(args);
   381	  } catch (e) {
   382	    if (e.refused) {
   383	      process.stderr.write(`\nAEGIS-STATE REFUSED\n  rule:   AEGIS-CONNECTOR-PLANE-VIOLATION\n  reason: ${e.message}\n`);
   384	      process.exit(EXIT_REFUSED);
   385	    }
   386	    throw e;
   387	  }
   388	  const text = JSON.stringify(snap, null, 2);
   389	  if (args.out) {
   390	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(args.out), { recursive: true });
   391	    // A `.js` target emits a global assignment rather than bare JSON. The
   392	    // dashboard has to work from file:// with no server — that is the whole
   393	    // point of an evidence view — and fetch() of a local JSON file is blocked
   394	    // by the file:// origin policy. A script tag is not. The content is
   395	    // identical either way; only the wrapper differs.
   396	    const isJs = args.out.endsWith('.js');
   397	    const body = isJs
   398	      ? `/* Generated by builder-control/aegis-state.cjs — do not edit by hand.\n   Every value here cites the artifact it came from. Regenerate with:\n     node builder-control/aegis-state.cjs --out builder-control/dashboard/state.js */\nwindow.AEGIS_STATE = ${text};\n`
   399	      : text + '\n';
   400	    fs.writeFileSync(args.out, body, 'utf8');
   401	    process.stderr.write(`[aegis-state] wrote ${rel(path.resolve(args.out))}\n`);
   402	  } else {
   403	    process.stdout.write(text + '\n');
   404	  }
   405	  process.exit(EXIT_PASS);
   406	}
   407	
   408	module.exports = { snapshot, projectConnectors, projectReviewers, projectEvents, deriveStages, REQUIRED_STAGES };
     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	/**
     3	 * knowledge-mirror.cjs — the human knowledge layer, with no authority.
     4	 *
     5	 * WHAT THIS IS
     6	 * Notion (or any comparable store) holds the Product Owner's readable memory:
     7	 * vision, requirements, decisions, known issues, release history. This module
     8	 * models that mirror's STATE and its DISAGREEMENTS with AEGIS.
     9	 *
    10	 * WHAT THIS IS NOT
    11	 * A source of engineering truth. Three mechanical guarantees:
    12	 *
    13	 *   1. SELF-CERTIFICATION IS REFUSED. A knowledge record that asserts tests
    14	 *      passed, a feature is verified, a checkpoint exists, or a gate was
    15	 *      satisfied is REFUSED at load — the same treatment a CONTROL-plane
    16	 *      connector gets. The moment an external document can say "verified", it
    17	 *      is the brain, and it will be wrong exactly when it matters most.
    18	 *
    19	 *   2. SYNC STATE IS SEPARATE FROM ENGINEERING STATE. "Engineering: VERIFIED,
    20	 *      Notion: SYNC FAILED" is a valid pair. A failed connector never
    21	 *      downgrades verified work, and a write that did not happen is never
    22	 *      rendered SYNCED.
    23	 *
    24	 *   3. CONFLICTS ARE NOT RESOLVED. When the mirror and AEGIS disagree, both
    25	 *      values are shown and a Product Owner decision is required. Silently
    26	 *      preferring either side destroys the only signal that something is wrong.
    27	 *
    28	 * NO NETWORK. This module performs no external calls. It reads a local mirror
    29	 * file and reports DISCONNECTED when there is nothing to read, which is the
    30	 * honest state of a connector that has never been authorized.
    31	 *
    32	 *   node builder-control/knowledge-mirror.cjs [--json] [--mirror <file>]
    33	 *
    34	 * Exit: 0 projected · 2 usage · 3 refused (self-certification attempt)
    35	 */
    36	'use strict';
    37	
    38	const fs = require('fs');
    39	const path = require('path');
    40	
    41	const HERE = __dirname;
    42	const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..');
    43	const MIRROR = path.join(HERE, 'knowledge-mirror.json');
    44	
    45	const EXIT_PASS = 0;
    46	const EXIT_USAGE = 2;
    47	const EXIT_REFUSED = 3;
    48	
    49	// The vocabulary. PENDING is not optimism — it means dispatched and
    50	// unconfirmed, which is different from succeeded.
    51	const SYNC_STATES = ['SYNCED', 'PENDING', 'STALE', 'FAILED', 'CONFLICT', 'DISCONNECTED'];
    52	
    53	// Claims a knowledge record may never make. Each of these is a control-plane
    54	// verdict; a document asserting one is claiming authority it does not have.
    55	const FORBIDDEN_CLAIMS = [
    56	  'testsPassed', 'verified', 'checkpointCreated', 'gateSatisfied',
    57	  'reviewBypassed', 'watchdogOverridden', 'policyAltered', 'releaseApproved',
    58	];
    59	
    60	class KnowledgeRefusal extends Error {}
    61	
    62	function readMirror(file) {
    63	  const p = file ? path.resolve(file) : MIRROR;
    64	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    65	    return {
    66	      state: 'DISCONNECTED',
    67	      reason: `no knowledge mirror at ${path.relative(ROOT, p)}. The Notion connector has never been authorized, so there is nothing to mirror.`,
    68	      records: [], source: path.relative(ROOT, p),
    69	    };
    70	  }
    71	  let raw;
    72	  try { raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8')); }
    73	  catch (e) {
    74	    return { state: 'FAILED', reason: `knowledge mirror unreadable: ${e.message}`, records: [], source: path.relative(ROOT, p) };
    75	  }
    76	  return { state: 'OK', raw, records: raw.records || [], source: path.relative(ROOT, p) };
    77	}
    78	
    79	// Refuse, do not sanitize. Stripping the offending field would let the record
    80	// through in a weakened form and teach whoever wrote it that the claim was
    81	// merely ignored rather than prohibited.
    82	function assertNoSelfCertification(records) {
    83	  for (const r of records) {
    84	    for (const claim of FORBIDDEN_CLAIMS) {
    85	      if (r[claim] !== undefined) {
    86	        throw new KnowledgeRefusal(
    87	          `knowledge record "${r.recordId || '(unnamed)'}" asserts "${claim}". ` +
    88	          'The knowledge mirror organizes human memory; it may not certify engineering state. ' +
    89	          'Remove the claim and let AEGIS evidence decide.'
    90	        );
    91	      }
    92	    }
    93	    if (typeof r.aegisClaim === 'string' && /^(VERIFIED|PASS|APPROVED)$/i.test(r.aegisClaim)) {
    94	      throw new KnowledgeRefusal(
    95	        `knowledge record "${r.recordId || '(unnamed)'}" sets aegisClaim="${r.aegisClaim}". ` +
    96	        'A knowledge record may state what the HUMAN believes (notionStatus), never what AEGIS concluded.'
    97	      );
    98	    }
    99	  }
   100	}
   101	
   102	function ageMinutes(iso, now) {
   103	  const t = Date.parse(iso);
   104	  return Number.isNaN(t) ? null : Math.floor((now - t) / 60000);
   105	}
   106	
   107	/**
   108	 * Compare what the human store believes against what AEGIS actually verified.
   109	 * `aegisVerified` is supplied by the caller from real gate output — this module
   110	 * never infers it.
   111	 */
   112	function reconcile(records, aegisVerifiedById, now, staleAfterMin = 1440) {
   113	  const rows = [];
   114	  for (const r of records) {
   115	    const notion = r.notionStatus || 'UNKNOWN';
   116	    const aegis = aegisVerifiedById && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(aegisVerifiedById, r.recordId)
   117	      ? aegisVerifiedById[r.recordId]
   118	      : 'NOT_VERIFIED';
   119	
   120	    let sync = SYNC_STATES.includes(r.syncState) ? r.syncState : 'DISCONNECTED';
   121	
   122	    // A write that was never confirmed is not a write.
   123	    if (sync === 'SYNCED' && !r.lastSyncedAt) {
   124	      sync = 'PENDING';
   125	    } else if (sync === 'SYNCED' && r.lastSyncedAt) {
   126	      const age = ageMinutes(r.lastSyncedAt, now);
   127	      if (age === null) sync = 'PENDING';
   128	      else if (age > staleAfterMin) sync = 'STALE';
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    // The disagreement that matters: the human store says done, AEGIS has no
   132	    // evidence. This is not resolved here and must not be.
   133	    const claimsDone = /^(DONE|COMPLETE|SHIPPED|VERIFIED)$/i.test(notion);
   134	    const conflict = claimsDone && aegis !== 'VERIFIED';
   135	    if (conflict) sync = 'CONFLICT';
   136	
   137	    rows.push({
   138	      recordId: r.recordId,
   139	      title: r.title || r.recordId,
   140	      notionStatus: notion,
   141	      aegisStatus: aegis,
   142	      syncState: sync,
   143	      lastSyncedAt: r.lastSyncedAt || null,
   144	      ageMinutes: r.lastSyncedAt ? ageMinutes(r.lastSyncedAt, now) : null,
   145	      conflict: conflict
   146	        ? {
   147	            kind: 'SOURCE-OF-TRUTH CONFLICT',
   148	            notion, aegis,
   149	            requiredAction: 'RECONCILE — Product Owner decision required. This is not resolved automatically in either direction.',
   150	          }
   151	        : null,
   152	      // Human proposals are inert until they enter the workflow.
   153	      proposal: r.proposedChange
   154	        ? { state: 'PROPOSED', detail: r.proposedChange,
   155	            note: 'A Product Owner change becomes a PROPOSED objective and enters the normal loop. It never modifies code directly and never skips a stage.' }
   156	        : null,
   157	    });
   158	  }
   159	  return rows;
   160	}
   161	
   162	function project(opts = {}) {
   163	  const now = typeof opts.now === 'number' ? opts.now : Date.now();
   164	  const m = readMirror(opts.mirror);
   165	  if (m.state === 'DISCONNECTED' || m.state === 'FAILED') {
   166	    return {
   167	      state: m.state, reason: m.reason, source: m.source,
   168	      records: [], conflicts: 0,
   169	      authorityNote: 'The knowledge mirror holds human memory and never engineering authority.',
   170	    };
   171	  }
   172	  assertNoSelfCertification(m.records);
   173	  const rows = reconcile(m.records, opts.aegisVerifiedById || {}, now, m.raw.staleAfterMinutes);
   174	  return {
   175	    state: 'OK',
   176	    source: m.source,
   177	    records: rows,
   178	    conflicts: rows.filter((r) => r.conflict).length,
   179	    syncStates: SYNC_STATES,
   180	    authorityNote: 'The knowledge mirror holds human memory and never engineering authority.',
   181	  };
   182	}
   183	
   184	// ── CLI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   185	if (require.main === module) {
   186	  const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
   187	  const opts = {};
   188	  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
   189	    if (argv[i] === '--mirror') opts.mirror = argv[++i];
   190	    else if (argv[i] === '--json') opts.json = true;
   191	  }
   192	  let code = EXIT_PASS;
   193	  try {
   194	    const out = project(opts);
   195	    if (opts.json) console.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 2));
   196	    else {
   197	      console.log('AEGIS — KNOWLEDGE MIRROR');
   198	      console.log('='.repeat(56));
   199	      console.log(`state  : ${out.state}`);
   200	      if (out.reason) console.log(`reason : ${out.reason}`);
   201	      console.log(`source : ${out.source}`);
   202	      if (out.records.length) {
   203	        console.log('');
   204	        for (const r of out.records) {
   205	          console.log(`  ${r.syncState.padEnd(12)} ${r.title}`);
   206	          console.log(`               notion=${r.notionStatus}  aegis=${r.aegisStatus}`);
   207	          if (r.conflict) {
   208	            console.log(`               ${r.conflict.kind}`);
   209	            console.log(`               ${r.conflict.requiredAction}`);
   210	          }
   211	        }
   212	        console.log('');
   213	        console.log(`conflicts: ${out.conflicts}`);
   214	      }
   215	      console.log('');
   216	      console.log(out.authorityNote);
   217	    }
   218	  } catch (e) {
   219	    if (e instanceof KnowledgeRefusal) {
   220	      process.stderr.write(`\nAEGIS-KNOWLEDGE-SELF-CERTIFICATION\n  ${e.message}\n\nNothing was projected. Refused rather than sanitized.\n`);
   221	      code = EXIT_REFUSED;
   222	    } else throw e;
   223	  }
   224	  process.exit(code);
   225	}
   226	
   227	module.exports = { project, reconcile, assertNoSelfCertification, readMirror, SYNC_STATES, FORBIDDEN_CLAIMS, KnowledgeRefusal };
