   220	<div class="sr" role="status" aria-live="polite" id="live"></div>
   221	
   222	<!-- Real state, generated by aegis-state.cjs. Loaded BEFORE the renderer so the
   223	     renderer sees either genuine state or genuinely nothing. If this file is
   224	     absent the page renders UNAVAILABLE everywhere, which is the intended and
   225	     tested failure path. -->
   226	<script src="state.js"></script>
   227	<script>
   228	(function(){
   229	  'use strict';
   230	  var S = window.AEGIS_STATE || null;
   231	  var $ = function(id){ return document.getElementById(id); };
   232	  var GLYPH = {
   233	    PASS:'●', HEALTHY:'●', AVAILABLE:'●', FRESH:'●',
   234	    ACTIVE:'◐', WARNING:'▲', DEGRADED:'▲', STALE:'◑',
   235	    FAILED:'✕', MISSING:'✕', BLOCKED:'■',
   236	    UNVERIFIED:'?', UNKNOWN:'?', NOT_REQUIRED:'–',
   237	    UNAVAILABLE:'⊘', DISCONNECTED:'⊘', AUTH_EXPIRED:'⊘',
   238	    // FINDING #9: exact verdicts and statuses are now rendered verbatim rather
   239	    // than collapsed, so every canonical value needs its own glyph. A readiness
   240	    // state is deliberately NOT given the PASS dot — it is a waypoint, not an
   241	    // outcome, and it must not look like one at a glance.
   242	    READY_FOR_DETERMINISTIC_VALIDATION:'◇', READY_FOR_PR:'◆',
   243	    INCOMPLETE:'◌', IMPORTED:'⇢',
   244	    SYNCED:'●', PENDING:'◌', CONFLICT:'⚑', REFUSED:'⊘', OK:'●'
   245	  };
   246	
   247	  function el(tag, cls, text){
   248	    var n = document.createElement(tag);
   249	    if (cls) n.className = cls;
   250	    if (text != null) n.textContent = text;
   251	    return n;
   252	  }
   253	  // Status is colour + label + glyph. Never colour alone (§J).
   254	  function chip(state){
   255	    var c = el('span','chip s-'+state);
   256	    c.appendChild(el('span','g', GLYPH[state] || '?'));
   257	    c.appendChild(el('span',null,state));
   258	    return c;
   259	  }
   260	
   261	  // No state at all: say so loudly rather than render an empty, confident page.
   262	  if (!S) {
   263	    $('fatal').appendChild((function(){
   264	      var d = el('div','note fatal');
   265	      d.appendChild(el('strong',null,'UNAVAILABLE — no AEGIS state is loaded.'));
   266	      d.appendChild(el('div',null,
   267	        'state.js is missing or failed to parse. Nothing on this page is being rendered from real state, so nothing on it should be believed. Regenerate with: node builder-control/aegis-state.cjs --out builder-control/dashboard/state.js'));
   268	      return d;
   269	    })());
   270	    $('ctx-verdict').appendChild(chip('UNAVAILABLE'));
   271	    return;
   272	  }
   273	
   274	  var eng = S.engineering || {};
   275	  $('ctx-generated').textContent = 'generated ' + (S.generatedAt || 'UNKNOWN');
   276	  $('ctx-subject').textContent = eng.subjectSha256
   277	    ? 'subject ' + String(eng.subjectSha256).slice(0,12) + '…'
   278	    : 'subject UNAVAILABLE';
   279	  // CONFIRMED FINDING #9: this used to render ANY non-BLOCKED verdict as
   280	  // "PASS", so READY_FOR_DETERMINISTIC_VALIDATION — which explicitly means the
   281	  // checks have NOT been run — displayed as a pass. The exact canonical verdict
   282	  // is now rendered verbatim. If the gate invents a new state tomorrow, this
   283	  // shows that state rather than guessing which side of the line it falls on.
   284	  $('ctx-verdict').appendChild(chip(eng.verdict || 'UNAVAILABLE'));
   285	
   286	  // ── stages ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   287	  var stages = (eng.stages || []);
   288	  var BREAKS = { MISSING:1, FAILED:1, BLOCKED:1, UNAVAILABLE:1 };
   289	  var list = $('stages');
   290	  if (!stages.length) {
   291	    list.appendChild(el('li','empty','UNAVAILABLE — no stage projection was produced.'));
   292	  }
   293	  stages.forEach(function(st, i){
   294	    var li = el('li');
   295	    var b = el('button','stage' + (BREAKS[st.state] ? ' broken' : '') + (st.state === 'ACTIVE' ? ' is-active' : ''));
   296	    b.type = 'button';
   297	    b.setAttribute('aria-label', st.label + ': ' + st.state + ' — ' + st.reason);
   298	
   299	    var rail = el('span','rail'); rail.appendChild(el('span','dot s-'+st.state)); rail.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
   300	    var mid = el('span'); mid.appendChild(el('span','label', st.label));
   301	    mid.appendChild(el('span','why', st.reason));
   302	    var badges = el('span','badges'); badges.appendChild(chip(st.state));
   303	
   304	    b.appendChild(rail); b.appendChild(mid); b.appendChild(badges);
   305	    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ select(b, 'stage', st); });
   306	    b.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
   307	      var items = Array.prototype.slice.call(list.querySelectorAll('.stage'));
   308	      var idx = items.indexOf(b);
   309	      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' && idx < items.length-1){ e.preventDefault(); items[idx+1].focus(); }
   310	      if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' && idx > 0){ e.preventDefault(); items[idx-1].focus(); }
   311	    });
   312	    li.appendChild(b); list.appendChild(li);
   313	  });
   314	
   315	  // Drift is stated in words, not implied by a colour.
   316	  var missing = stages.filter(function(s){ return s.state === 'MISSING'; });
   317	  if (missing.length){
   318	    var d = el('div','note');
   319	    d.appendChild(el('strong',null,'PROCESS DRIFT — verification blocked.'));
   320	    d.appendChild(el('div',null,'Required stage(s) with no evidence bound to this subject: '
   321	      + missing.map(function(m){ return m.label; }).join(', ')
   322	      + '. The path above is severed at each one. This is the gate’s own verdict, not a second opinion.'));
   323	    $('drift').appendChild(d);
   324	  }
   325	
   326	  // ── knowledge mirror ────────────────────────────────────────────
   327	  // Its own plane. A CONFLICT shows BOTH values and resolves nothing —
   328	  // preferring either side would destroy the only signal that they disagree.
   329	  var kn = S.knowledge || null;
   330	  var kl = $('knowledge');
   331	  if (!kn || kn.state !== 'OK'){
   332	    kl.appendChild(el('li','empty', (kn ? kn.state : 'UNAVAILABLE') + ' — '
   333	      + ((kn && kn.reason) || 'no knowledge mirror is configured')));
   334	  } else if (!(kn.records||[]).length){
   335	    kl.appendChild(el('li','empty','No knowledge records.'));
   336	  } else {
   337	    kn.records.forEach(function(r){
   338	      var li = el('li','row');
   339	      var left = el('div');
   340	      left.appendChild(el('div','name', r.title));
   341	      left.appendChild(el('div','meta', 'human: ' + r.notionStatus
   342	        + ' · aegis: ' + r.aegisStatus
   343	        + (r.conflict ? ' · ' + r.conflict.requiredAction : '')));
   344	      var right = el('div','badges');
   345	      right.appendChild(chip(r.syncState));
   346	      li.appendChild(left); li.appendChild(right);
   347	      kl.appendChild(li);
   348	    });
   349	  }
   350	
   351	  // ── connectors ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   352	  var conn = (S.integration && S.integration.connectors) || {};
   353	  var cl = $('connectors');
   354	  if (conn.state !== 'OK'){
   355	    cl.appendChild(el('li','empty','UNAVAILABLE — ' + (conn.reason || 'connector registry could not be read')));
   356	  } else if (!(conn.connectors||[]).length){
   357	    cl.appendChild(el('li','empty','No connectors are configured.'));
   358	  } else {
   359	    conn.connectors.forEach(function(c){
   360	      var li = el('li','row');
   361	      var left = el('div');
   362	      var nm = el('div','name', c.label + (c.legacy ? ' — LEGACY/OPTIONAL' : ''));
   363	      left.appendChild(nm);
   364	      left.appendChild(el('div','meta', c.provider + ' · ' + c.executionPath
   365	        + ' · auth ' + c.authStatus));
   366	      var right = el('div','badges');
   367	      right.appendChild(chip(c.health));
   368	      // Staleness qualifies health; it never overwrites or hides it.
   369	      if (c.staleness && c.staleness.state !== 'FRESH'){
   370	        var s = chip(c.staleness.state);
   371	        if (c.staleness.ageMinutes != null) s.lastChild.textContent = 'STALE ' + c.staleness.ageMinutes + 'm';
   372	        right.appendChild(s);
   373	      }
   374	      li.appendChild(left); li.appendChild(right);
   375	      li.tabIndex = 0; li.style.cursor='pointer';
   376	      li.addEventListener('click', function(){ select(li,'connector', c); });
   377	      li.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter'){ select(li,'connector', c); } });
   378	      cl.appendChild(li);
   379	    });
   380	  }
   381	
   382	  // ── reviewers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
   383	  var rv = S.reviewers || {}; var rl = $('reviewers');
   384	  if (rv.state !== 'OK'){
   385	    rl.appendChild(el('li','empty','UNAVAILABLE — ' + (rv.reason || 'reviewer state could not be read')));
   386	  } else {
   387	    (rv.reviewers||[]).forEach(function(r){
   388	      var li = el('li','row');
   389	      var left = el('div');
   390	      left.appendChild(el('div','name', r.label || r.toolId));
   391	      left.appendChild(el('div','meta', r.role));
   392	      var right = el('div','badges');
   393	      right.appendChild(chip(r.availability));
   394	      // Available to run is not the same as having run. Say which.
   395	      var st = stages.filter(function(s){
   396	        return (r.toolId === 'codex-local' && s.id === 'codex') || (r.toolId === 'grok-cli' && s.id === 'grok');
   397	      })[0];
   398	      if (st) right.appendChild(chip(st.state || 'UNKNOWN'));
   399	      li.appendChild(left); li.appendChild(right);
   400	      li.tabIndex = 0; li.style.cursor='pointer';
   401	      li.addEventListener('click', function(){ select(li,'reviewer', r); });
   402	      li.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter'){ select(li,'reviewer', r); } });
   403	      rl.appendChild(li);
   404	    });
   405	  }
   406	
   407	  // ── events ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   408	  var ev = S.events || {}; var evl = $('events');
   409	  if (ev.state !== 'OK'){
   410	    evl.appendChild(el('li','empty','UNAVAILABLE — ' + (ev.reason || 'ledger could not be read')));
   411	  } else {
   412	    (ev.events||[]).forEach(function(e){
   413	      var li = el('li','row');
   414	      var left = el('div');
   415	      left.appendChild(el('div','name', e.gate + (e.blockRule ? ' · ' + e.blockRule : '')));
   416	      left.appendChild(el('div','meta mono', e.ts));
   417	      // CONFIRMED FINDING #9: every non-PASS ledger event was rendered
   418	      // "BLOCKED", which turned a FAILED integration operation into what looked
   419	      // like a governance refusal. Those are opposite diagnoses — one means a
   420	      // connector broke, the other means a rule stopped you. The exact status
   420	      // connector broke, the other means a rule stopped you. The exact status
   421	      // is now shown.
   422	      var right = el('div','badges'); right.appendChild(chip(e.status || 'UNKNOWN'));
   423	      li.appendChild(left); li.appendChild(right); evl.appendChild(li);
   424	    });
   425	  }
   426	
   427	  // ── inspector ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
   428	  var current = null;
   429	  function select(node, kind, data){
   430	    if (current) current.removeAttribute('aria-current');
   431	    current = node; node.setAttribute('aria-current','true');
   432	    var box = $('inspector'); box.textContent = '';
   433	    var dl = el('dl','kv');
   434	    function kv(k,v){ dl.appendChild(el('dt',null,k)); dl.appendChild(el('dd',null, v == null ? '—' : String(v))); }
   435	
   436	    if (kind === 'stage'){
   437	      box.appendChild(el('h3',null,data.label));
   438	      kv('State', data.state); kv('Reason', data.reason);
   439	      kv('Stage id', data.id);
   440	      box.appendChild(dl);
   441	      box.appendChild(prov(eng.source, 'derived from the gate verdict; this view re-expresses it and decides nothing'));
   442	    } else if (kind === 'connector'){
   443	      box.appendChild(el('h3',null,data.label));
   444	      kv('Plane', data.plane + ' — worker, never engineering authority');
   445	      kv('Health', data.health);
   446	      kv('Staleness', data.staleness ? (data.staleness.state + (data.staleness.ageMinutes!=null ? ' ('+data.staleness.ageMinutes+'m)' : '')) : '—');
   447	      kv('Auth', data.authStatus);
   448	      kv('Execution path', data.executionPath);
   449	      kv('Supports', (data.capabilities||[]).join(', ') || '—');
   450	      kv('Declared NOT supported', (data.declaredNotSupported||[]).join(', ') || '—');
   451	      kv('Failures', data.failureCount);
   452	      kv('Risk', data.riskLevel);
   453	      if (data.evidence){ kv('Observed at', data.evidence.observedAt); kv('Method', data.evidence.method); kv('Result', data.evidence.result); }
   454	      else kv('Evidence','UNVERIFIED — never probed');
   455	      if (data.authorityNote) kv('Authority', data.authorityNote);
   456	      box.appendChild(dl); box.appendChild(prov(data.source));
   457	    } else {
   458	      box.appendChild(el('h3',null,data.label || data.toolId));
   459	      kv('Role', data.role); kv('Availability', data.availability);
   460	      if (data.evidence){ kv('Observed at', data.evidence.observedAt); kv('Method', data.evidence.method); kv('Result', data.evidence.result); }
   461	      else kv('Evidence','UNVERIFIED');
   462	      kv('Note','Available to run is not the same as having run. A review counts only when a record is bound to this subject.');
   463	      box.appendChild(dl); box.appendChild(prov(data.source));
   464	    }
   465	    $('live').textContent = (data.label || data.toolId) + ' selected.';
   466	  }
   467	  function prov(src, extra){
   468	    var p = el('div','prov');
   469	    p.appendChild(document.createTextNode('Source: '));
   470	    p.appendChild(el('code',null, src || 'UNKNOWN'));
   471	    if (extra) p.appendChild(el('div',null, extra));
   472	    return p;
   473	  }
   474	
   475	  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
   476	    if (e.key === 'Escape' && current){
   477	      current.removeAttribute('aria-current'); current = null;
   478	      $('inspector').textContent = '';
   479	      $('inspector').appendChild(el('p','empty','Select a stage, connector or reviewer.'));
   480	    }
   481	  });
   482	
   483	  if (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(min-width:1920px)').matches){
   484	    document.body.setAttribute('data-mode','command');
   485	  }
   486	})();
   487	</script>
   488	</body>
   489	</html>
     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	/**
     3	 * dashboard-slice.test.cjs — proves the visual slice cannot lie.
     4	 *
     5	 * These are static assertions over the slice's source. They are deliberately
     6	 * crude and deliberately paranoid, because the failure they guard against is
     7	 * not a crash — it is a dashboard that looks healthy while the system is not.
     8	 * A timer-driven pulse, a hardcoded "PASS", or a seeded fallback object would
     9	 * all render beautifully and mean nothing.
    10	 *
    11	 * Browser behaviour (real render, failure path, contrast, responsive) is
    12	 * verified separately and recorded in the evidence report; these tests hold the
    13	 * invariants that must never regress unnoticed between those sessions.
    14	 */
    15	'use strict';
    16	
    17	const assert = require('assert');
    18	const fs = require('fs');
    19	const path = require('path');
    20	
    21	const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');
    22	const DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'builder-control', 'dashboard');
    23	const HTML_PATH = path.join(DIR, 'index.html');
    24	const STATE_PATH = path.join(DIR, 'state.js');
    25	
    26	let passed = 0;
    27	function test(name, fn) {
    28	  try { fn(); passed++; console.log(`ok   ${name}`); }
    29	  catch (e) { console.error(`FAIL ${name}: ${e.message}`); process.exitCode = 1; }
    30	}
    31	
    32	const html = fs.readFileSync(HTML_PATH, 'utf8');
    33	// Strip comments so a rule that *describes* a ban is not mistaken for breaking it.
    34	const code = html.replace(/<!--[\s\S]*?-->/g, '').replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '');
    35	
    36	console.log('AEGIS dashboard slice — invariants');
    37	
    38	// ── nothing may animate on a timer ──────────────────────────────────────────
    39	test('no timers anywhere: a pulse that can run without a real event is fake activity', () => {
    40	  for (const banned of ['setInterval', 'setTimeout', 'requestAnimationFrame']) {
    41	    assert.ok(!new RegExp('\\b' + banned + '\\s*\\(').test(code),
    42	      `${banned}( found in executable code — motion must be bound to state, not to time`);
    43	  }
    44	});
    45	
    46	test('the only animation is bound to a real ACTIVE state', () => {
    47	  const keyframes = code.match(/@keyframes\s+([\w-]+)/g) || [];
    48	  assert.strictEqual(keyframes.length, 1, `expected exactly 1 keyframes block, found ${keyframes.length}`);
    49	  assert.ok(/\.stage\.is-active\s+\.dot\s*\{[^}]*animation/.test(code),
    50	    'the single animation must be gated on .is-active, which is set only from state');
    51	});
    52	
    53	test('reduced motion is honoured and removes the animation', () => {
    54	  assert.ok(/@media\s*\(prefers-reduced-motion:\s*reduce\)/.test(code), 'no reduced-motion block');
    55	  const block = code.slice(code.indexOf('prefers-reduced-motion'));
    56	  assert.ok(/animation\s*:\s*none/.test(block.slice(0, 400)), 'reduced motion must disable the animation');
    57	});
    58	
    59	// ── one data path, no seeds ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    60	test('the slice reads window.AEGIS_STATE and has no fallback seed object', () => {
    61	  assert.ok(/window\.AEGIS_STATE/.test(code), 'must read the projector output');
    62	  assert.ok(!/AEGIS_STATE\s*\|\|\s*\{/.test(code),
    63	    'a `|| {}` fallback would let the page render confidently with no real state');
    64	  assert.ok(/AEGIS_STATE\s*\|\|\s*null/.test(code), 'absence must resolve to null and be handled explicitly');
    65	});
    66	
    67	test('state.js is generated, never hand-authored', () => {
    68	  assert.ok(fs.existsSync(STATE_PATH), 'state.js missing — regenerate with aegis-state.cjs --out');
    69	  const s = fs.readFileSync(STATE_PATH, 'utf8');
    70	  assert.ok(/Generated by builder-control\/aegis-state\.cjs/.test(s), 'state.js lacks its generator header');
    71	  assert.ok(/do not edit by hand/i.test(s));
    72	  const json = JSON.parse(s.slice(s.indexOf('{'), s.lastIndexOf('}') + 1));
    73	  assert.deepStrictEqual(json.contract.absences, ['UNAVAILABLE', 'STALE', 'UNVERIFIED']);
    74	});
    75	
    76	test('state.js loads BEFORE the renderer, or the renderer would always see nothing', () => {
    77	  // Search the comment-stripped source. The file's own header comment mentions
    78	  // window.AEGIS_STATE while explaining the rule, and matching that prose
    79	  // instead of the executable read made this test fail on correct code.
    80	  const stateTag = code.indexOf('<script src="state.js">');
    81	  const renderer = code.indexOf('var S = window.AEGIS_STATE');
    82	  assert.ok(stateTag !== -1, 'state.js is never loaded');
    83	  assert.ok(renderer !== -1, 'the renderer no longer reads window.AEGIS_STATE');
    84	  assert.ok(stateTag < renderer,
    85	    `state.js (@${stateTag}) must load before the renderer reads it (@${renderer})`);
    86	});
    87	
    88	// ── no fabricated values ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    89	test('no hardcoded status word appears in the markup', () => {
    90	  const body = html.slice(html.indexOf('<body'));
    91	  const markup = body.replace(/<script[\s\S]*?<\/script>/g, '').replace(/<!--[\s\S]*?-->/g, '');
    92	  for (const word of ['PASS', 'HEALTHY', 'VERIFIED', 'COMPLETE']) {
    93	    assert.ok(!new RegExp('>\\s*' + word).test(markup),
    94	      `the literal "${word}" appears in static markup — every status must come from state`);
    95	  }
    96	});
    97	
    98	test('no fabricated progress, percentage, uptime or KPI vocabulary', () => {
    99	  for (const banned of ['percentComplete', 'progressPercent', 'uptime', 'Math.random', 'toFixed(0) + \'%\'']) {
   100	    assert.ok(!code.includes(banned), `"${banned}" found — the slice must not synthesise numbers`);
   101	  }
   102	  assert.ok(!/\b\d{1,3}%\s*<\//.test(html), 'a literal percentage is rendered in markup');
   103	});
   104	
   105	// ── the three honest absences are all reachable ─────────────────────────────
   106	test('UNAVAILABLE, STALE and UNVERIFIED each have a rendering', () => {
   107	  for (const s of ['UNAVAILABLE', 'STALE', 'UNVERIFIED']) {
   108	    assert.ok(code.includes(s), `${s} has no rendering path`);
   109	    assert.ok(new RegExp('\\.s-' + s + '\\b').test(code) || new RegExp('s-\\$\\{?').test(code),
   110	      `${s} has no state style`);
   111	  }
   112	  assert.ok(/UNAVAILABLE — no AEGIS state is loaded/.test(code),
   113	    'the no-state failure path must be explicit, not an empty page');
   114	});
   115	
   116	test('status is never colour-only — every chip carries a glyph and a label', () => {
   117	  assert.ok(/GLYPH\s*=/.test(code), 'no glyph map');
   118	  assert.ok(/c\.appendChild\(el\('span',null,state\)\)/.test(code),
   119	    'the chip must append the state name as text, not rely on colour');
   120	});
   121	
   122	// ── 2D is the product ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   123	test('no WebGL, no 3D library, no canvas — 2D is authoritative, not a fallback', () => {
   124	  for (const banned of ['webgl', 'three.js', 'THREE.', '<canvas', 'getContext(']) {
   125	    assert.ok(!code.toLowerCase().includes(banned.toLowerCase()),
   126	      `"${banned}" found — the V1 slice must be fully operational without any 3D layer`);
   127	  }
   128	});
   129	
   130	test('zero external requests: no CDN, no remote font, no fetch', () => {
   131	  assert.ok(!/https?:\/\//.test(code.replace(/xmlns="[^"]*"/g, '')),
   132	    'an absolute URL is referenced — the evidence view must work offline from file://');
   133	  assert.ok(!/\bfetch\s*\(/.test(code), 'fetch() is blocked at file:// origin; state must arrive by script tag');
   134	});
   135	
   136	// ── accessibility invariants ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   137	test('keyboard and assistive-tech affordances are present', () => {
   138	  assert.ok(/aria-live="polite"/.test(html), 'no live region');
   139	  assert.ok(/aria-label/.test(code), 'stages carry no accessible label');
   140	  assert.ok(/:focus-visible/.test(code), 'no visible focus style');
   141	  assert.ok(/Escape/.test(code), 'no keyboard dismiss');
   142	  assert.ok(/ArrowDown/.test(code) && /ArrowUp/.test(code), 'no arrow-key traversal along the path');
   143	  assert.ok(/class="sr"[^>]*href="#topology"/.test(html), 'no skip link');
   144	});
   145	
   146	test('every state colour token clears 3:1 on the panel background', () => {
   147	  const vars = {};
   148	  const rootBlock = code.slice(code.indexOf(':root{'), code.indexOf('}', code.indexOf(':root{')));
   149	  for (const m of rootBlock.matchAll(/(--[\w-]+):\s*(#[0-9a-fA-F]{6})/g)) vars[m[1]] = m[2];
   150	  const lum = (h) => {
   151	    const c = h.slice(1).match(/../g).map((x) => {
   152	      const v = parseInt(x, 16) / 255;
   153	      return v <= 0.03928 ? v / 12.92 : Math.pow((v + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
   154	    });
   155	    return 0.2126 * c[0] + 0.7152 * c[1] + 0.0722 * c[2];
   156	  };
   157	  const ratio = (a, b) => { const l1 = lum(a), l2 = lum(b); const hi = Math.max(l1, l2), lo = Math.min(l1, l2); return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05); };
   158	  const bg = vars['--bg-1'];
   159	  assert.ok(bg, 'no --bg-1 token');
   160	  for (const t of ['--pass', '--active', '--warn', '--fail', '--blocked', '--stale', '--unknown', '--text-0', '--text-1']) {
   161	    assert.ok(vars[t], `token ${t} missing`);
   162	    const r = ratio(vars[t], bg);
   163	    assert.ok(r >= 3, `${t} (${vars[t]}) is ${r.toFixed(2)}:1 on ${bg} — below the 3:1 this design requires`);
   164	  }
   165	});
   166	
   167	const failedCount = process.exitCode ? 'at least 1' : '0';
   168	console.log(`${passed} passed, ${failedCount} failed.`);
     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	/**
     3	 * aegis-state.test.cjs — red proofs for the state projector.
     4	 *
     5	 * The projector is the only thing the dashboard reads, which makes it the exact
     6	 * place a comfortable lie would enter the system. Every case below asserts an
     7	 * uncomfortable output: a refusal, an UNKNOWN, a STALE, an UNAVAILABLE. A
     8	 * projector that quietly upgraded any of these would pass a happy-path suite
     9	 * and fail the only job it has.
    10	 */
    11	'use strict';
    12	
    13	const assert = require('assert');
    14	const fs = require('fs');
    15	const os = require('os');
    16	const path = require('path');
    17	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    18	
    19	const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');
    20	const CLI = path.join(ROOT, 'builder-control', 'aegis-state.cjs');
    21	const REGISTRY = path.join(ROOT, 'builder-control', 'connector-registry.json');
    22	const M = require('../aegis-state.cjs');
    23	
    24	let passed = 0;
    25	function test(name, fn) {
    26	  try { fn(); passed++; console.log(`ok   ${name}`); }
    27	  catch (e) { console.error(`FAIL ${name}: ${e.message}`); process.exitCode = 1; }
    28	}
    29	
    30	const NOW = Date.parse('2026-08-23T12:00:00Z');
    31	console.log('AEGIS state projector — red proofs');
    32	
    33	// ── the authority boundary is mechanical, not advisory ──────────────────────
    34	test('a connector claiming CONTROL plane is REFUSED, not warned about', () => {
    35	  const original = fs.readFileSync(REGISTRY, 'utf8');
    36	  const reg = JSON.parse(original);
    37	  reg.connectors.push({
    38	    connectorId: 'rogue', label: 'Rogue', provider: 'x',
    39	    plane: 'CONTROL', health: 'HEALTHY',
    40	    healthEvidence: { observedAt: new Date().toISOString(), method: 'm', result: 'r' },
    41	  });
    42	  fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, JSON.stringify(reg, null, 2));
    43	  try {
    44	    const r = spawnSync('node', [CLI], { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' });
    45	    assert.strictEqual(r.status, 3, `expected refusal exit 3, got ${r.status}`);
    46	    assert.ok(/AEGIS-CONNECTOR-PLANE-VIOLATION/.test(r.stderr), 'must name the rule');
    47	    assert.ok(/never hold engineering authority/.test(r.stderr), 'must state why');
    48	  } finally {
    49	    fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, original);
    50	  }
    51	  // and the restore must have worked, or every later test is meaningless
    52	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(REGISTRY, 'utf8'), original);
    53	});
    54	
    55	// ── no optimistic defaults ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    56	test('a connector with no health evidence is UNKNOWN, never HEALTHY', () => {
    57	  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'aegis-reg-'));
    58	  const p = path.join(dir, 'connector-registry.json');
    59	  fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify({
    60	    connectors: [{ connectorId: 'ghost', plane: 'INTEGRATION', health: 'HEALTHY' }],
    61	  }));
    62	  // exercise the pure function against a registry with no evidence
    63	  const original = fs.readFileSync(REGISTRY, 'utf8');
    64	  fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
    65	  try {
    66	    const out = M.projectConnectors(NOW);
    67	    const g = out.connectors[0];
    68	    assert.strictEqual(g.health, 'UNKNOWN', 'a claimed HEALTHY with no evidence must degrade to UNKNOWN');
    69	    assert.strictEqual(g.staleness.state, 'UNVERIFIED');
    70	  } finally { fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, original); fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
    71	});
    72	
    73	test('stale evidence is reported with its age and does NOT overwrite last-known health', () => {
    74	  const original = fs.readFileSync(REGISTRY, 'utf8');
    75	  fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, JSON.stringify({
    76	    stalenessThresholdMinutes: 60,
    77	    healthVocabulary: ['HEALTHY', 'UNKNOWN'],
    78	    connectors: [{
    79	      connectorId: 'aged', plane: 'INTEGRATION', health: 'HEALTHY',
    80	      healthEvidence: { observedAt: '2026-08-23T06:00:00Z', method: 'm', result: 'r' },
    81	    }],
    82	  }));
    83	  try {
    84	    const c = M.projectConnectors(NOW).connectors[0];
    85	    assert.strictEqual(c.staleness.state, 'STALE');
    86	    assert.strictEqual(c.staleness.ageMinutes, 360, 'age must be computed, not asserted');
    87	    // Overwriting health would destroy the only reading we have; hiding the age
    88	    // would let a six-hour-old reading pose as live. Both are required.
    89	    assert.strictEqual(c.health, 'HEALTHY', 'last-known health is preserved and qualified, not erased');
    90	  } finally { fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, original); }
    91	});
    92	
    93	test('fresh evidence inside the threshold is FRESH', () => {
    94	  const original = fs.readFileSync(REGISTRY, 'utf8');
    95	  fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, JSON.stringify({
    96	    stalenessThresholdMinutes: 60,
    97	    healthVocabulary: ['HEALTHY'],
    98	    connectors: [{ connectorId: 'new', plane: 'INTEGRATION', health: 'HEALTHY',
    99	      healthEvidence: { observedAt: '2026-08-23T11:30:00Z', method: 'm', result: 'r' } }],
   100	  }));
   101	  try {
   102	    const c = M.projectConnectors(NOW).connectors[0];
   103	    assert.strictEqual(c.staleness.state, 'FRESH');
   104	    assert.strictEqual(c.staleness.ageMinutes, 30);
   105	  } finally { fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, original); }
   106	});
   107	
   108	test('a missing registry renders UNAVAILABLE rather than an empty healthy list', () => {
   109	  const original = fs.readFileSync(REGISTRY, 'utf8');
   110	  fs.unlinkSync(REGISTRY);
   111	  try {
   112	    const out = M.projectConnectors(NOW);
   113	    assert.strictEqual(out.state, 'UNAVAILABLE');
   114	    assert.ok(/not found/.test(out.reason));
   115	    assert.deepStrictEqual(out.connectors, []);
   116	  } finally { fs.writeFileSync(REGISTRY, original); }
   117	});
   118	
   119	// ── failure isolation is structural ─────────────────────────────────────────
   120	test('engineering and integration are separate objects with no blended health field', () => {
   121	  const snap = M.snapshot({});
   122	  assert.ok(snap.engineering, 'engineering missing');
   123	  assert.ok(snap.integration, 'integration missing');
   124	  // The moment a combined field exists, somebody lets a failed connector
   125	  // darken a verified build. There must be nowhere to put that.
   126	  for (const k of ['overallHealth', 'systemHealth', 'health', 'status', 'score', 'percentComplete']) {
   127	    assert.ok(!(k in snap), `snapshot must not expose a blended "${k}" field`);
   128	  }
   129	  assert.ok(!('engineering' in snap.integration) && !('integration' in snap.engineering),
   130	    'the two planes must not nest inside each other');
   131	});
   132	
   133	// ── stage derivation never invents a pass ───────────────────────────────────
   134	test('a required reviewer with no bound record derives MISSING, not PASS', () => {
   135	  const stages = M.deriveStages({
   136	    ok: false,
   137	    classification: { lane: 'FULL', highRisk: true, requiredReviewers: ['codex', 'grok'] },
   138	    problems: [
   139	      { rule: 'ENGOS-REVIEW-MISSING', detail: 'codex is required for lane FULL/high-risk and has no valid review record' },
   140	      { rule: 'ENGOS-REVIEW-MISSING', detail: 'grok is required for lane FULL/high-risk and has no valid review record' },
   141	    ],
   142	    observed: [],
   143	  });
   144	  const by = Object.fromEntries(stages.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
   145	  assert.strictEqual(by.codex.state, 'MISSING');
   146	  assert.strictEqual(by.grok.state, 'MISSING');
   147	  assert.strictEqual(by.gate.state, 'BLOCKED');
   148	});
   149	
   150	test('runtime and checkpoint are ALWAYS UNVERIFIED — this gate cannot speak to them', () => {
   151	  const stages = M.deriveStages({
   152	    ok: true, state: 'READY_FOR_PR',
   153	    classification: { lane: 'FULL', highRisk: false, requiredReviewers: [] },
   154	    problems: [], observed: ['deterministic checks executed here: 3/3 passed'],
   155	  });
   156	  const by = Object.fromEntries(stages.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
   157	  // Even on a fully passing gate, these must not read PASS: nothing ran them.
   158	  assert.strictEqual(by.runtime.state, 'UNVERIFIED');
   159	  assert.strictEqual(by.checkpoint.state, 'UNVERIFIED');
   160	  assert.strictEqual(by.copilot.state, 'UNVERIFIED', 'copilot entitlement is unproven; it may never read PASS');
   161	});
   162	
   163	test('deterministic checks read UNVERIFIED when the gate did not run them itself', () => {
   164	  const stages = M.deriveStages({
   165	    ok: false, classification: { lane: 'FULL', highRisk: false, requiredReviewers: [] },
   166	    problems: [], observed: ['lane FULL'],
   167	  });
   168	  const d = stages.find((s) => s.id === 'deterministic');
   169	  assert.strictEqual(d.state, 'UNVERIFIED');
   170	  assert.ok(/not evidence of it/.test(d.reason), 'must say why passing elsewhere is not evidence here');
   171	});
   172	
   173	test('a reviewer not required for the lane is NOT_REQUIRED, distinct from PASS', () => {
   174	  const stages = M.deriveStages({
   175	    ok: true, state: 'READY_FOR_DETERMINISTIC_VALIDATION',
   176	    classification: { lane: 'LIGHT', highRisk: false, requiredReviewers: [] },
   177	    problems: [], observed: [],
   178	  });
   179	  const by = Object.fromEntries(stages.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
   180	  assert.strictEqual(by.grok.state, 'NOT_REQUIRED');
   181	  assert.notStrictEqual(by.grok.state, 'PASS');
   182	});
   183	
   184	// ── provenance ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   185	test('every projected section cites the artifact it came from', () => {
   186	  const snap = M.snapshot({});
   187	  assert.ok(snap.engineering.source, 'engineering has no source');
   188	  assert.ok(snap.integration.connectors.connectors.every((c) => !!c.source), 'a connector has no source');
   189	  assert.ok(snap.reviewers.reviewers.every((r) => !!r.source), 'a reviewer has no source');
   190	  assert.ok(snap.events.source || snap.events.state !== 'OK', 'events have no source');
   191	});
   192	
   193	test('the snapshot declares its three honest absences', () => {
   194	  const snap = M.snapshot({});
   195	  assert.deepStrictEqual(snap.contract.absences, ['UNAVAILABLE', 'STALE', 'UNVERIFIED']);
   196	});
   197	
   198	test('the ledger is the event source — no second event store is introduced', () => {
   199	  const snap = M.snapshot({});
   200	  assert.ok(/ledger\.json$/.test(snap.events.source || ''), 'events must come from the existing ledger');
   201	  assert.ok(typeof snap.events.total === 'number' && snap.events.total > 0);
   202	});
   203	
   204	// ── CODEX REVIEW CYCLE 1 — confirmed finding #8 ────────────────────────────
   205	test('finding #8: build is NOT PASS merely because a diff exists', () => {
   206	  const stages = M.deriveStages({
   207	    ok: false,
   208	    classification: { lane: 'FULL', highRisk: false, requiredReviewers: [] },
   209	    subject: { subjectPaths: ['src/a.ts'] },
   210	    problems: [], observed: ['lane FULL'],
   211	  });
   212	  const b = stages.find((s) => s.id === 'build');
   213	  assert.strictEqual(b.state, 'UNVERIFIED', 'a diff existing is not evidence that it builds');
   214	  assert.ok(/nothing in this verdict verifies it builds/.test(b.reason));
   215	});

   219	    ok: false, classification: { lane: 'FULL', highRisk: false, requiredReviewers: [] },
   220	    subject: { subjectPaths: [] }, problems: [], observed: [],
   221	  });

   225	test('finding #8: a required reviewer does NOT default to PASS on an unrecognised rule', () => {
   226	  // A rule the stage mapper does not specifically recognise (stale binding,
   227	  // short coverage, ambiguity) previously fell through to PASS.
   228	  const stages = M.deriveStages({
   229	    ok: false,
   230	    classification: { lane: 'FULL', highRisk: true, requiredReviewers: ['codex', 'grok'] },
   231	    subject: { subjectPaths: ['src/a.ts'] },
   232	    problems: [{ rule: 'ENGOS-REVIEW-COVERAGE-SHORT', detail: 'REV-1 (codex) did not cover 4 subject path(s)' }],
   233	    observed: [],
   234	  });
   235	  const by = Object.fromEntries(stages.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
   236	  assert.notStrictEqual(by.codex.state, 'PASS', 'an unrecognised failure must never render as an approval');
   237	  assert.strictEqual(by.codex.state, 'FAILED');
   238	  assert.strictEqual(by.grok.state, 'UNVERIFIED', 'no observed approval and no matching problem = UNVERIFIED');
   239	});

   244	    classification: { lane: 'FULL', highRisk: false, requiredReviewers: ['codex'] },
   245	    subject: { subjectPaths: ['src/a.ts'] },
   246	    problems: [], observed: ['codex: APPROVE'],
   247	  });

   249	});

    81	
    82	function ageMinutes(iso, now) {
    83	  const t = Date.parse(iso);
    84	  if (Number.isNaN(t)) return null;
    85	  return Math.floor((now - t) / 60000);
    86	}
    87	
    88	// ── connectors ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    89	function projectConnectors(now) {
    90	  if (!fs.existsSync(REGISTRY)) {
    91	    return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `connector registry not found at ${rel(REGISTRY)}`, connectors: [] };
    92	  }
    93	  let reg;
    94	  try { reg = readJSON(REGISTRY); }
    95	  catch (e) { return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `connector registry unreadable: ${e.message}`, connectors: [] }; }
    96	
    97	  const threshold = Number(reg.stalenessThresholdMinutes) > 0 ? Number(reg.stalenessThresholdMinutes) : 60;
    98	  const vocab = new Set(reg.healthVocabulary || []);
    99	  const connectors = [];
   100	
   101	  for (const c of reg.connectors || []) {
   102	    // Refuse, do not warn. A connector claiming control-plane standing is the
   103	    // one thing this whole amendment exists to make impossible.
   104	    if (c.plane !== 'INTEGRATION') {
   105	      const err = new Error(
   106	        `connector "${c.connectorId}" declares plane "${c.plane}". Only INTEGRATION-plane connectors may be loaded. ` +
   107	        'External systems execute actions; they never hold engineering authority.'
   108	      );
   109	      err.refused = true;
   110	      throw err;
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    // An unprobed connector is UNKNOWN. Never HEALTHY by omission.
   114	    let health = vocab.has(c.health) ? c.health : 'UNKNOWN';
   115	    const ev = c.healthEvidence || null;
   116	    let staleness = null;
   117	    if (!ev || !ev.observedAt) {
   118	      health = 'UNKNOWN';
   119	      staleness = { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no dated health evidence has ever been recorded' };
   120	    } else {
   121	      const age = ageMinutes(ev.observedAt, now);
   122	      if (age === null) {
   123	        health = 'UNKNOWN';
   124	        staleness = { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: `unparseable observedAt "${ev.observedAt}"` };
   125	      } else if (age > threshold) {
   126	        // Staleness does NOT overwrite the last known health — it qualifies it.
   127	        // Overwriting would destroy the only information we have; hiding it
   128	        // would let an hours-old reading pose as live.
   129	        staleness = { state: 'STALE', ageMinutes: age, thresholdMinutes: threshold,
   130	          reason: `last observed ${age} minute(s) ago, threshold ${threshold}` };
   131	      } else {
   132	        staleness = { state: 'FRESH', ageMinutes: age };
   133	      }
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    connectors.push({
   137	      connectorId: c.connectorId,
   138	      label: c.label || c.connectorId,
   139	      provider: c.provider || 'UNKNOWN',
   140	      plane: c.plane,
   141	      executionPath: c.executionPath || 'UNKNOWN',
   142	      legacy: c.status === 'LEGACY_OPTIONAL',
   143	      health,
   144	      staleness,
   145	      authStatus: c.authStatus || 'UNKNOWN',
   146	      capabilities: c.capabilities || [],
   147	      declaredNotSupported: c.declaredNotSupported || [],
   148	      failureCount: typeof c.failureCount === 'number' ? c.failureCount : null,
   149	      lastSuccess: c.lastSuccess || null,
   150	      lastFailure: c.lastFailure || null,
   151	      riskLevel: c.riskLevel || 'UNKNOWN',
   152	      evidence: ev ? { observedAt: ev.observedAt, method: ev.method, result: ev.result } : null,
   153	      authorityNote: c.authorityNote || null,
   154	      source: rel(REGISTRY),
   155	    });
   156	  }
   157	  return { state: 'OK', thresholdMinutes: threshold, connectors };
   158	}
   159	
   160	// ── reviewers (from the tool canon — availability with dated evidence) ──────
   161	function projectReviewers(now) {
   162	  if (!fs.existsSync(CANON)) {
   163	    return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `tool canon not found at ${rel(CANON)}`, reviewers: [] };
   164	  }
   165	  let canon;
   166	  try { canon = readJSON(CANON); }
   167	  catch (e) { return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `tool canon unreadable: ${e.message}`, reviewers: [] }; }
   168	
   169	  const wanted = { 'codex-local': 'independent review', 'grok-cli': 'adversarial red team' };
   170	  const reviewers = [];
   171	  for (const [toolId, role] of Object.entries(wanted)) {
   172	    const t = (canon.tools || []).find((x) => x.toolId === toolId);
   173	    if (!t) {
   174	      reviewers.push({ toolId, role, availability: 'UNAVAILABLE',
   175	        reason: 'not present in the Tool Capability Canon', evidence: null, source: rel(CANON) });
   176	      continue;
   177	    }
   178	    const ev = t.availabilityEvidence || null;
   179	    reviewers.push({
   180	      toolId, role,
   181	      label: t.label || toolId,
   182	      availability: t.availability || 'UNKNOWN',
   183	      enabled: t.enabled !== false,
   184	      evidence: ev ? { observedAt: ev.observedAt, method: ev.method, result: ev.result } : null,
   185	      evidenceAgeMinutes: ev && ev.observedAt ? ageMinutes(ev.observedAt, now) : null,
   186	      source: rel(CANON),
   187	    });
   188	  }
   189	  return { state: 'OK', reviewers };
   190	}
   191	
   192	// ── engineering state (the gate's own verdict — never re-judged here) ───────
   193	function projectEngineering(args) {
   194	  const a = [ENGOS, '--gate-done', '--json'];
   195	  if (args.base) a.push('--base', args.base);
   196	  if (args.head) a.push('--head', args.head);
   197	  if (args.diffLines) a.push('--diff-lines', String(args.diffLines));
   198	  for (const c of args.changed || []) a.push('--changed', c);
   199	  if (args.packet) a.push('--packet', args.packet);
   200	  if (args.subjectSha) a.push('--subject-sha', args.subjectSha);
   201	  const r = spawnSync('node', a, { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
   202	
   203	  // A BLOCKED gate exits 3. That is a successful reading of a blocked system,
   204	  // not a failure to read — so it is projected, not swallowed.
   205	  let parsed = null;
   206	  try { parsed = JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { /* handled below */ }
   207	  if (!parsed) {
   208	    return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE',
   209	      reason: `could not read a gate verdict (exit ${r.status}): ${(r.stderr || '').trim().split('\n')[0] || 'no output'}`,
   210	      source: rel(ENGOS) };
   211	  }
   212	
   213	  const stageEvidence = deriveStages(parsed);
   214	  return {
   215	    state: 'OK',
   216	    gateExit: r.status,
   217	    verdict: parsed.ok ? parsed.state : 'BLOCKED',
   218	    lane: parsed.classification ? parsed.classification.lane : 'UNKNOWN',
   219	    highRisk: parsed.classification ? !!parsed.classification.highRisk : null,
   220	    requiredReviewers: parsed.classification ? parsed.classification.requiredReviewers : [],
   221	    subjectSha256: parsed.subject ? parsed.subject.subjectSha256 : null,
   222	    subjectPaths: parsed.subject ? parsed.subject.subjectPaths : [],
   223	    excludedAsEvidence: parsed.subject ? parsed.subject.excludedAsEvidence : [],
   224	    problems: parsed.problems || [],
   225	    observed: parsed.observed || [],
   226	    unverified: parsed.unverified || [],
   227	    stages: stageEvidence,
   228	    source: rel(ENGOS),
   229	  };
   230	}
   231	
   232	// Map the gate verdict onto the declared stage sequence. This does not decide
   233	// anything — it re-expresses the gate's own problems as stage states so the
   234	// drift is drawable. A stage with no positive evidence is UNVERIFIED, which is
   235	// distinct from FAILED: nobody looked, versus somebody looked and it failed.
   236	function deriveStages(parsed) {
   237	  const problems = parsed.problems || [];
   238	  const observed = (parsed.observed || []).join(' | ');
   239	  const has = (re) => problems.some((p) => re.test(p.rule + ' ' + p.detail));
   240	
   241	  const stageFor = (id) => {
   242	    switch (id) {
   243	      case 'spec':
   244	        if (has(/ENGOS-SPEC-UNPINNED/)) return { state: 'FAILED', reason: 'a source of truth is neither pinned nor marked UNVERIFIED' };
   245	        if (/spec pin:/.test(observed)) return { state: 'PASS', reason: observed.match(/spec pin: [^|]*/)[0].trim() };
   246	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no spec pin was evaluated' };
   247	      case 'build':
   248	        // CONFIRMED FINDING (codex REV-…-codex, CRITICAL/HIGH set, #8): this
   249	        // returned PASS unconditionally. A diff existing is not evidence that
   250	        // it builds — nothing in a gate verdict speaks to compilation at all.
   251	        // Rendering PASS here was the projector fabricating a positive state,
   252	        // which is precisely what the amendment forbids.
   253	        if (!parsed.subject || !(parsed.subject.subjectPaths || []).length) {
   254	          return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: 'no subject paths — there is nothing here to build' };
   255	        }
   256	        if (/deterministic checks executed here: (\d+)\/\1 passed/.test(observed)) {
   257	          return { state: 'PASS', reason: 'the gate executed the declared checks and they passed' };
   258	        }
   259	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'a subject diff exists, but nothing in this verdict verifies it builds' };
   260	      case 'deterministic':
   261	        if (has(/ENGOS-DETERMINISTIC-FAILED/)) return { state: 'FAILED', reason: 'a declared check failed' };
   262	        if (/deterministic checks executed here/.test(observed)) return { state: 'PASS', reason: 'checks executed by the gate' };
   263	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'the gate did not run the checks (no --run-checks); they may have passed elsewhere, but this is not evidence of it' };
   264	      case 'codex':
   265	      case 'grok': {
   266	        const req = (parsed.classification && parsed.classification.requiredReviewers) || [];
   267	        if (!req.includes(id)) return { state: 'NOT_REQUIRED', reason: `not required for lane ${parsed.classification ? parsed.classification.lane : '?'}` };
   268	        if (has(new RegExp(`ENGOS-REVIEWER-UNAVAILABLE.*${id}|${id}.*UNAVAILABLE`, 'i'))) return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: 'the reviewer could not run' };
   269	        if (has(new RegExp(`ENGOS-REVIEW-REJECTED.*${id}`, 'i'))) return { state: 'FAILED', reason: 'the reviewer returned REJECT' };
   270	        if (has(new RegExp(`ENGOS-REVIEW-MISSING.*\\b${id}\\b`, 'i'))) return { state: 'MISSING', reason: 'required, and no review record is bound to this subject' };
   271	        // CONFIRMED FINDING (#8): this used to fall through to PASS after
   272	        // checking only three failure shapes. Any rule the checks above did not
   273	        // recognise — a stale binding, short coverage, ambiguous records, a
   274	        // wrong packet — silently rendered as an approval. Absence of a known
   275	        // failure is not evidence of a pass. Require the gate to have positively
   276	        // observed this reviewer's disposition instead.
   277	        if (new RegExp(`(^|\\|)\\s*${id}: (APPROVE|APPROVE_WITH_NOTES)`, 'm').test(observed)) {
   278	          return { state: 'PASS', reason: 'the gate observed an approval bound to this subject' };
   279	        }
   280	        if (problems.some((pr) => new RegExp(id, 'i').test(pr.rule + ' ' + pr.detail))) {
   281	          const hit = problems.find((pr) => new RegExp(id, 'i').test(pr.rule + ' ' + pr.detail));
   282	          return { state: 'FAILED', reason: hit.rule + ': ' + String(hit.detail).slice(0, 120) };
   283	        }
   284	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no approval was observed for this reviewer on this subject' };
   285	      }
   286	      case 'copilot':
   287	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'advisory reviewer; entitlement unproven and no record collected' };
   288	      case 'gate':
   289	        return parsed.ok
   290	          ? { state: 'PASS', reason: parsed.state }
   291	          : { state: 'BLOCKED', reason: `${problems.length} unmet rule(s)` };
   292	      case 'runtime':
   293	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'runtime validation is a separate step and has not run' };
   294	      case 'checkpoint':
   295	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'no checkpoint is claimed by this gate verdict' };
   296	      default:
   297	        return { state: 'UNVERIFIED', reason: 'unmapped stage' };
   298	    }
   299	  };
   300	
   301	  return REQUIRED_STAGES.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, label: s.label, ...stageFor(s.id) }));
   302	}
   303	
   304	// ── recent events (the ledger IS the event bus — no second store) ───────────
   305	function projectEvents(limit) {
   306	  if (!fs.existsSync(LEDGER)) {
   307	    return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `ledger not found at ${rel(LEDGER)}`, events: [] };
   308	  }
   309	  let l;
   310	  try { l = readJSON(LEDGER); }
   311	  catch (e) { return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: `ledger unreadable: ${e.message}`, events: [] }; }
   312	  if (!Array.isArray(l)) return { state: 'UNAVAILABLE', reason: 'ledger is not an array', events: [] };
   313	  const events = l.slice(-limit).reverse().map((e) => ({
   314	    entryId: e.entryId, ts: e.ts, gate: e.gate, status: e.status,
   315	    agentId: e.agentId, packetId: e.packetId || null,
   316	    blockRule: e.blockRule || null,
   317	    operation: e.operation || null,
   318	  }));
   319	  return { state: 'OK', total: l.length, events, source: rel(LEDGER) };
   320	}

   322	// ── snapshot ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   323	function snapshot(args) {
   324	  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
   325	  const now = Date.parse(nowIso);
   326	  const engineering = projectEngineering(args);
   327	  const integration = { connectors: projectConnectors(now) };
   328	
   329	  // Failure isolation, made structural: these are two objects. There is no
   330	  // combined "overall health" field, because the moment one exists somebody
   260	
   261	  // No state at all: say so loudly rather than render an empty, confident page.
   262	  if (!S) {
   263	    $('fatal').appendChild((function(){
   264	      var d = el('div','note fatal');
   265	      d.appendChild(el('strong',null,'UNAVAILABLE — no AEGIS state is loaded.'));
   266	      d.appendChild(el('div',null,
   267	        'state.js is missing or failed to parse. Nothing on this page is being rendered from real state, so nothing on it should be believed. Regenerate with: node builder-control/aegis-state.cjs --out builder-control/dashboard/state.js'));
   268	      return d;
   269	    })());
   270	    $('ctx-verdict').appendChild(chip('UNAVAILABLE'));
   271	    return;
   272	  }
   273	
   274	  var eng = S.engineering || {};
   275	  $('ctx-generated').textContent = 'generated ' + (S.generatedAt || 'UNKNOWN');
   276	  $('ctx-subject').textContent = eng.subjectSha256
   277	    ? 'subject ' + String(eng.subjectSha256).slice(0,12) + '…'
   278	    : 'subject UNAVAILABLE';
   279	  // CONFIRMED FINDING #9: this used to render ANY non-BLOCKED verdict as
   280	  // "PASS", so READY_FOR_DETERMINISTIC_VALIDATION — which explicitly means the
   281	  // checks have NOT been run — displayed as a pass. The exact canonical verdict
   282	  // is now rendered verbatim. If the gate invents a new state tomorrow, this
   283	  // shows that state rather than guessing which side of the line it falls on.
   284	  $('ctx-verdict').appendChild(chip(eng.verdict || 'UNAVAILABLE'));
   285	
   286	  // ── stages ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   287	  var stages = (eng.stages || []);
   288	  var BREAKS = { MISSING:1, FAILED:1, BLOCKED:1, UNAVAILABLE:1 };
   289	  var list = $('stages');
   290	  if (!stages.length) {
   291	    list.appendChild(el('li','empty','UNAVAILABLE — no stage projection was produced.'));
   292	  }
   293	  stages.forEach(function(st, i){
   294	    var li = el('li');
   295	    var b = el('button','stage' + (BREAKS[st.state] ? ' broken' : '') + (st.state === 'ACTIVE' ? ' is-active' : ''));
   296	    b.type = 'button';
   297	    b.setAttribute('aria-label', st.label + ': ' + st.state + ' — ' + st.reason);
   298	
   299	    var rail = el('span','rail'); rail.appendChild(el('span','dot s-'+st.state)); rail.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
   300	    var mid = el('span'); mid.appendChild(el('span','label', st.label));
   301	    mid.appendChild(el('span','why', st.reason));
   302	    var badges = el('span','badges'); badges.appendChild(chip(st.state));
   303	
   304	    b.appendChild(rail); b.appendChild(mid); b.appendChild(badges);
   305	    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ select(b, 'stage', st); });
   306	    b.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
   307	      var items = Array.prototype.slice.call(list.querySelectorAll('.stage'));
   308	      var idx = items.indexOf(b);
   309	      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' && idx < items.length-1){ e.preventDefault(); items[idx+1].focus(); }
   310	      if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' && idx > 0){ e.preventDefault(); items[idx-1].focus(); }
   311	    });
   312	    li.appendChild(b); list.appendChild(li);
   313	  });
   314	
   315	  // Drift is stated in words, not implied by a colour.
   316	  var missing = stages.filter(function(s){ return s.state === 'MISSING'; });
   317	  if (missing.length){
   318	    var d = el('div','note');
   319	    d.appendChild(el('strong',null,'PROCESS DRIFT — verification blocked.'));
   320	    d.appendChild(el('div',null,'Required stage(s) with no evidence bound to this subject: '
   321	      + missing.map(function(m){ return m.label; }).join(', ')
   322	      + '. The path above is severed at each one. This is the gate’s own verdict, not a second opinion.'));
   323	    $('drift').appendChild(d);
   324	  }
   325	
   326	  // ── knowledge mirror ────────────────────────────────────────────
   327	  // Its own plane. A CONFLICT shows BOTH values and resolves nothing —
   328	  // preferring either side would destroy the only signal that they disagree.
   329	  var kn = S.knowledge || null;
   330	  var kl = $('knowledge');
   331	  if (!kn || kn.state !== 'OK'){
   332	    kl.appendChild(el('li','empty', (kn ? kn.state : 'UNAVAILABLE') + ' — '
   333	      + ((kn && kn.reason) || 'no knowledge mirror is configured')));
   334	  } else if (!(kn.records||[]).length){
   335	    kl.appendChild(el('li','empty','No knowledge records.'));
   336	  } else {
   337	    kn.records.forEach(function(r){
   338	      var li = el('li','row');
   339	      var left = el('div');
   340	      left.appendChild(el('div','name', r.title));
   341	      left.appendChild(el('div','meta', 'human: ' + r.notionStatus
   342	        + ' · aegis: ' + r.aegisStatus
   343	        + (r.conflict ? ' · ' + r.conflict.requiredAction : '')));
   344	      var right = el('div','badges');
   345	      right.appendChild(chip(r.syncState));
   346	      li.appendChild(left); li.appendChild(right);
   347	      kl.appendChild(li);
   348	    });
   349	  }
   350	
   351	  // ── connectors ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   352	  var conn = (S.integration && S.integration.connectors) || {};
   353	  var cl = $('connectors');
   354	  if (conn.state !== 'OK'){
   355	    cl.appendChild(el('li','empty','UNAVAILABLE — ' + (conn.reason || 'connector registry could not be read')));
   356	  } else if (!(conn.connectors||[]).length){
   357	    cl.appendChild(el('li','empty','No connectors are configured.'));
   358	  } else {
   359	    conn.connectors.forEach(function(c){
   360	      var li = el('li','row');
   361	      var left = el('div');
   362	      var nm = el('div','name', c.label + (c.legacy ? ' — LEGACY/OPTIONAL' : ''));
   363	      left.appendChild(nm);
   364	      left.appendChild(el('div','meta', c.provider + ' · ' + c.executionPath
   365	        + ' · auth ' + c.authStatus));
   366	      var right = el('div','badges');
   367	      right.appendChild(chip(c.health));
   368	      // Staleness qualifies health; it never overwrites or hides it.
   369	      if (c.staleness && c.staleness.state !== 'FRESH'){
   370	        var s = chip(c.staleness.state);
   371	        if (c.staleness.ageMinutes != null) s.lastChild.textContent = 'STALE ' + c.staleness.ageMinutes + 'm';
   372	        right.appendChild(s);
   373	      }
   374	      li.appendChild(left); li.appendChild(right);
   375	      li.tabIndex = 0; li.style.cursor='pointer';

   442	    } else if (kind === 'connector'){
   443	      box.appendChild(el('h3',null,data.label));
   444	      kv('Plane', data.plane + ' — worker, never engineering authority');
   445	      kv('Health', data.health);
   446	      kv('Staleness', data.staleness ? (data.staleness.state + (data.staleness.ageMinutes!=null ? ' ('+data.staleness.ageMinutes+'m)' : '')) : '—');
   447	      kv('Auth', data.authStatus);
   448	      kv('Execution path', data.executionPath);
   449	      kv('Supports', (data.capabilities||[]).join(', ') || '—');
   450	      kv('Declared NOT supported', (data.declaredNotSupported||[]).join(', ') || '—');
   451	      kv('Failures', data.failureCount);
   452	      kv('Risk', data.riskLevel);
   453	      if (data.evidence){ kv('Observed at', data.evidence.observedAt); kv('Method', data.evidence.method); kv('Result', data.evidence.result); }
   454	      else kv('Evidence','UNVERIFIED — never probed');
   455	      if (data.authorityNote) kv('Authority', data.authorityNote);
   456	      box.appendChild(dl); box.appendChild(prov(data.source));
   457	    } else {

   459	      kv('Role', data.role); kv('Availability', data.availability);
   460	      if (data.evidence){ kv('Observed at', data.evidence.observedAt); kv('Method', data.evidence.method); kv('Result', data.evidence.result); }

   463	      box.appendChild(dl); box.appendChild(prov(data.source));
   464	    }
   465	    $('live').textContent = (data.label || data.toolId) + ' selected.';
   466	  }
   467	  function prov(src, extra){

   472	    return p;
   473	  }

   480	    }
   481	  });
