    54	      "required": [
    55	        "diffSha256",
    56	        "changedPaths"
    57	      ],
    58	      "properties": {
    59	        "diffSha256": {
    60	          "type": "string",
    61	          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$",
    62	          "description": "SHA-256 of the SUBJECT diff that was reviewed, from `engineering-os.cjs --subject`. The subject excludes review evidence, so adding this record does not change the hash it names."
    63	        },
    64	        "baseRef": {
    65	          "type": "string",
    66	          "description": "Base ref/commit the diff was taken against."
    67	        },
    68	        "headRef": {
    69	          "type": "string",
    70	          "description": "Head ref/commit reviewed."
    71	        },
    72	        "changedPaths": {
    73	          "type": "array",
    74	          "minItems": 1,
    75	          "items": {
    76	            "type": "string",
    77	            "minLength": 1
    78	          },
    79	          "description": "REQUIRED. Every subject path this reviewer actually read. The gate requires exact set equality with the subject: a reviewer that saw 3 of 11 files and returned APPROVE is otherwise indistinguishable from one that saw everything."
    80	        }
    81	      },
    82	      "additionalProperties": false
    83	    },
    84	    "disposition": {
    85	      "type": "string",
    86	      "enum": [
    87	        "APPROVE",
    88	        "APPROVE_WITH_NOTES",
    89	        "REJECT",
    90	        "UNAVAILABLE"
    91	      ],
    92	      "description": "Exactly one verdict. UNAVAILABLE means the reviewer could not be reached or run — it is reported as UNAVAILABLE and never silently upgraded. For Copilot and Grok, whose native output is PASS/FAIL prose, map REPOSITORY PASS / RED TEAM PASS to APPROVE and the FAIL forms to REJECT when transcribing into this record."
    93	    },
    94	    "unavailableReason": {
    95	      "type": "string",
    96	      "description": "Required in practice when disposition is UNAVAILABLE: the concrete reason (no credential, no network, tool not installed, quota). The gate prints this rather than inventing a cause."
    97	    },
    98	    "findings": {
    99	      "type": "array",
   100	      "description": "Structured findings. Empty array is valid and means 'no findings' — which is different from 'not reviewed' (that is disposition UNAVAILABLE).",
   101	      "items": {
   102	        "type": "object",
   103	        "required": [
   104	          "severity",
   105	          "problem",
   106	          "evidence",
   107	          "status"
   108	        ],
   109	        "properties": {
   110	          "severity": {
   111	            "type": "string",
   112	            "enum": [
   113	              "CRITICAL",
   114	              "HIGH",
   115	              "MEDIUM",
   116	              "LOW",
   117	              "INFORMATIONAL",
   118	              "FALSE_POSITIVE"
   119	            ],
   120	            "description": "CRITICAL and HIGH block while status is OPEN. FALSE_POSITIVE is the disposition for a finding the builder disputed and documented; it never blocks, and recording one requires the dispute note in `builderResponse`."
   121	          },
   122	          "file": {
   123	            "type": "string",
   124	            "description": "Repo-relative path the finding is in."
   125	          },
   126	          "location": {
   127	            "type": "string",
   128	            "description": "Line, range, symbol, or function."
   129	          },
   130	          "problem": {
   131	            "type": "string",
   132	            "description": "What is wrong."
   133	          },
   134	          "impact": {
   135	            "type": "string",
   136	            "description": "What breaks for a user or the system if it ships. REQUIRED (non-empty) for CRITICAL and HIGH."
   137	          },
   138	          "evidence": {
   139	            "type": "string",
   140	            "minLength": 1,
   141	            "description": "REQUIRED and non-empty: the quoted code, failing command, or contradicted spec line. A finding with no evidence is an opinion and the schema refuses it."
   142	          },
   143	          "requiredCorrection": {
   144	            "type": "string",
   145	            "description": "What must change. REQUIRED (non-empty) for CRITICAL and HIGH."
   146	          },
   147	          "verificationMethod": {
   148	            "type": "string",
   149	            "description": "The command or observation that proves the correction worked. REQUIRED (non-empty) for CRITICAL and HIGH."
   150	          },
   151	          "status": {
   152	            "type": "string",
   153	            "enum": [
   154	              "OPEN",
   155	              "FIXED",
   156	              "DISPUTED",
   157	              "ACCEPTED_RISK"
   158	            ],
   159	            "description": "OPEN blocks at CRITICAL/HIGH. FIXED requires verifiedByReviewId naming a non-self reviewer record for this same subject. DISPUTED requires builderResponse. ACCEPTED_RISK requires acceptedBy (a human)."
   160	          },
   161	          "builderResponse": {
   162	            "type": "string",
   163	            "description": "Required when status is DISPUTED or severity is FALSE_POSITIVE: why the builder believes the finding is wrong."
   164	          },
   165	          "acceptedBy": {
   166	            "type": "string",
   167	            "description": "Required when status is ACCEPTED_RISK: the human who accepted it. An AI may not accept risk on the Product Owner's behalf."
   168	          },
   169	          "verifiedByReviewId": {
   170	            "type": "string",
   171	            "description": "REQUIRED when status is FIXED: the reviewId of a record that re-verified the fix. It must be an active record bound to this same subject and must not be from 'claude-self' — a builder may not certify its own fix."
   172	          }
   173	        },
   174	        "additionalProperties": false
   175	      }
   176	    },
   177	    "unverified": {
   178	      "type": "array",
   179	      "items": {
   180	        "type": "string"
   181	      },
   182	      "description": "Things this reviewer could NOT check. Recorded so 'approved' is never mistaken for 'fully verified'."
   183	    },
   184	    "notes": {
   185	      "type": "string",
   186	      "description": "Free text. Not parsed by the gate."
   187	    },
   188	    "supersedes": {
   189	      "type": "string",
   190	      "description": "The reviewId this record replaces. This is the ONE deterministic replacement rule in the system: without it, two records from the same reviewer for the same subject are AMBIGUOUS and block. Resolution is never by timestamp or file order, because that would let whichever record sorted later silently overrule a rejection."
   191	    },
   192	    "attestation": {
   193	      "type": "object",
   194	      "description": "REQUIRED in practice: the gate refuses any record without a verifying attestation. It is an HMAC over the subject hash, packet authorization digest, reviewer identity, model, disposition and findings — so editing any of those after signing is detected. SCOPE: this proves the record was produced by this machine's adapter and is unmodified. It does NOT prove a human approved anything, and it does not defend against a process that can read the machine-local key.",
   195	      "required": [
   196	        "v",
   197	        "alg",
   198	        "mac",
   199	        "payloadDigest",
   200	        "signedAt"
   201	      ],
   202	      "properties": {
   203	        "v": {
   204	          "type": "string",
   205	          "description": "Attestation format version."
   206	        },
   207	        "alg": {
   208	          "type": "string",
   209	          "description": "MAC algorithm, e.g. HMAC-SHA256."
   210	        },
   211	        "mac": {
   212	          "type": "string",
   213	          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$",
   214	          "description": "The MAC itself."
   215	        },
   216	        "packetDigest": {
   217	          "type": [
   218	            "string",
   219	            "null"
   220	          ],
   221	          "description": "Digest of the packet's authorizing content at signing time. If the packet's filesAllowed or authorization changes afterwards, the review no longer describes what it was granted under and verification fails."
   222	        },
   223	        "payloadDigest": {
   224	          "type": "string",
   225	          "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$"
   226	        },
   227	        "signedAt": {
   228	          "type": "string"
   229	        },
   230	        "keySource": {
   231	          "type": "string",
   232	          "description": "Where the signing key came from. The key itself is never recorded."
   233	        },
   234	        "scope": {
   235	          "type": "string",
   236	          "description": "A plain statement of what this attestation does and does not prove."
   237	        }
   238	      },
   239	      "additionalProperties": false
   240	    },
   241	    "group": {
   242	      "type": "object",
   243	      "description": "Present on a per-group record from a chunked review. Group records live in reviews/groups/ and are NOT read by the gate — only a signed aggregate is.",
   244	      "required": [
   245	        "groupId"
   246	      ],
   247	      "properties": {
   248	        "groupId": {
   249	          "type": "string"
   250	        },
   251	        "groupDigest": {
   252	          "type": [
   253	            "string",
   254	            "null"
   255	          ]
   256	        }
   257	      },
   258	      "additionalProperties": false
   259	    },
   260	    "aggregate": {
   261	      "type": "object",
   262	      "description": "Present on an aggregate verdict built from per-group records. It is consumable only when the union of its groups covers the subject exactly, every group is signed and bound to the same subject, and one reviewer and model produced all of them. Each group's attestation digest is embedded so editing a group after aggregation invalidates the aggregate.",
   263	      "required": [
   264	        "groupCount",
   265	        "groups",
   266	        "coverage"
   267	      ],
   268	      "properties": {
   269	        "groupCount": {
   270	          "type": "integer",
   271	          "minimum": 0
   272	        },
   273	        "plannedGroupCount": {
   274	          "type": "integer",
   275	          "minimum": 0
   276	        },
   277	        "coverage": {
   278	          "type": "string",
   279	          "description": "EXACT, or the coverage failure code."
   280	        },
   281	        "groups": {
   282	          "type": "array",
   283	          "items": {
   284	            "type": "object",
   285	            "properties": {
   286	              "groupId": {
   287	                "type": [
   288	                  "string",
   289	                  "null"
   290	                ]
   291	              },
   292	              "groupDigest": {
   293	                "type": [
   294	                  "string",
   295	                  "null"
   296	                ]
   297	              },
   298	              "pathCount": {
   299	                "type": "integer"
   300	              },
   301	              "disposition": {
   302	                "type": "string"
   303	              },
   304	              "reviewId": {
   305	                "type": "string"
   306	              },
   307	              "attestationDigest": {

   311	                ]

   313	            },

   315	          }
   316	        },

     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	/**
     3	 * review-sign.test.cjs — red proofs for review attestation.
     4	 *
     5	 * The hole being closed: the gate used to trust any JSON in reviews/, so the
     6	 * builder could satisfy its own independent-review requirement by writing a
     7	 * file. Every case below asserts that a forged or altered record is REFUSED.
     8	 */
     9	'use strict';
    10	const assert = require('assert');
    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const os = require('os');
    13	const path = require('path');
    14	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    15	
    16	const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');
    17	const BC = path.join(ROOT, 'builder-control');
    18	const ENGOS = path.join(BC, 'engineering-os.cjs');
    19	const S = require('../review-sign.cjs');
    20	
    21	let passed = 0;
    22	function test(n, fn) {
    23	  try { fn(); passed++; console.log(`ok   ${n}`); }
    24	  catch (e) { console.error(`FAIL ${n}: ${e.message}`); process.exitCode = 1; }
    25	}
    26	const TMP = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'aegis-sign-'));
    27	process.on('exit', () => { try { fs.rmSync(TMP, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch {} });
    28	
    29	const PACKET = path.join(BC, 'packets', 'ENGINEERING-OS-V1.json');
    30	const PACKET_ID = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(PACKET, 'utf8')).packetId;
    31	const SHA = 'a'.repeat(64);
    32	
    33	const record = (over = {}) => ({
    34	  reviewId: 'REV-sign-001',
    35	  ts: '2026-08-24T12:00:00Z',
    36	  reviewer: 'codex',
    37	  reviewerModel: 'codex-cli-fixture',
    38	  packetId: PACKET_ID,
    39	  reviewOf: { diffSha256: SHA, changedPaths: ['src/app.ts'] },
    40	  disposition: 'APPROVE',
    41	  findings: [],
    42	  ...over,
    43	});
    44	
    45	console.log('AEGIS review attestation — red proofs');
    46	
    47	test('a signed record verifies', () => {
    48	  const signed = S.sign(record(), { packetPath: PACKET });
    49	  assert.ok(signed.attestation && signed.attestation.mac);
    50	  assert.strictEqual(S.verify(signed, { packetPath: PACKET }).ok, true);
    51	});
    52	
    53	test('RED: an UNSIGNED record is refused — this is the forged-approval case', () => {
    54	  const r = S.verify(record(), { packetPath: PACKET });
    55	  assert.strictEqual(r.ok, false);
    56	  assert.strictEqual(r.code, 'ATTESTATION-MISSING');
    57	  assert.ok(/anyone can write JSON/.test(r.reason));
    58	});
    59	
    60	for (const [field, mutate] of [
    61	  ['disposition', (r) => { r.disposition = 'APPROVE'; }],
    62	  ['reviewer', (r) => { r.reviewer = 'grok'; }],
    63	  ['reviewerModel', (r) => { r.reviewerModel = 'something-else'; }],
    64	  ['subject hash', (r) => { r.reviewOf.diffSha256 = 'b'.repeat(64); }],
    65	  ['changedPaths', (r) => { r.reviewOf.changedPaths = ['src/app.ts', 'src/extra.ts']; }],
    66	]) {
    67	  test(`RED: editing ${field} after signing invalidates the attestation`, () => {
    68	    const signed = S.sign(record({ disposition: 'REJECT' }), { packetPath: PACKET });
    69	    assert.strictEqual(S.verify(signed, { packetPath: PACKET }).ok, true, 'baseline must verify');
    70	    mutate(signed);
    71	    const r = S.verify(signed, { packetPath: PACKET });
    72	    assert.strictEqual(r.ok, false, `${field} was edited and still verified`);
    73	    assert.strictEqual(r.code, 'ATTESTATION-INVALID');
    74	  });
    75	}
    76	
    77	test('RED: downgrading a finding severity after signing is detected', () => {
    78	  const signed = S.sign(record({
    79	    disposition: 'REJECT',
    80	    findings: [{ severity: 'CRITICAL', file: 'a.js', problem: 'p', evidence: 'e',
    81	                 impact: 'i', requiredCorrection: 'c', verificationMethod: 'v', status: 'OPEN' }],
    82	  }), { packetPath: PACKET });
    83	  signed.findings[0].severity = 'LOW';
    84	  assert.strictEqual(S.verify(signed, { packetPath: PACKET }).code, 'ATTESTATION-INVALID');
    85	});
    86	
    87	test('RED: flipping a finding to FIXED after signing is detected', () => {
    88	  const signed = S.sign(record({
    89	    disposition: 'REJECT',
    90	    findings: [{ severity: 'HIGH', file: 'a.js', problem: 'p', evidence: 'e',
    91	                 impact: 'i', requiredCorrection: 'c', verificationMethod: 'v', status: 'OPEN' }],
    92	  }), { packetPath: PACKET });
    93	  signed.findings[0].status = 'FIXED';
    94	  assert.strictEqual(S.verify(signed, { packetPath: PACKET }).code, 'ATTESTATION-INVALID');
    95	});
    96	
    97	test('RED: widening the packet authorization after signing invalidates the review', () => {
    98	  // Finding #3: a review bound only to a packetId is not bound to what that
    99	  // packet permitted, and packets are excluded from the subject hash — so an
   100	  // edit here moves nothing the gate would otherwise notice.
   101	  const pktCopy = path.join(TMP, 'packet.json');
   102	  const pkt = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(PACKET, 'utf8'));
   103	  fs.writeFileSync(pktCopy, JSON.stringify(pkt));
   104	  const signed = S.sign(record(), { packetPath: pktCopy });
   105	  assert.strictEqual(S.verify(signed, { packetPath: pktCopy }).ok, true);
   106	
   107	  pkt.filesAllowed = [...pkt.filesAllowed, 'src/**', '**/*'];
   108	  fs.writeFileSync(pktCopy, JSON.stringify(pkt));
   109	  const r = S.verify(signed, { packetPath: pktCopy });
   110	  assert.strictEqual(r.ok, false);
   111	  assert.strictEqual(r.code, 'ATTESTATION-PACKET-CHANGED');
   112	});
   113	
   114	test('the attestation states its own limits rather than overclaiming', () => {
   115	  const signed = S.sign(record(), { packetPath: PACKET });
   116	  assert.ok(/does NOT prove human approval/i.test(signed.attestation.scope),
   117	    'an over-claimed control is worse than a missing one — the scope must say what it cannot do');
   118	});
   119	
   120	test('RED: the gate REFUSES an unsigned record dropped into reviews/', () => {
   121	  // End-to-end: the exact forgery this closes.
   122	  const dir = path.join(BC, 'reviews');
   123	  const created = !fs.existsSync(dir);
   124	  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   125	  const forged = path.join(dir, `.test-forged-${process.pid}.json`);
   126	  fs.writeFileSync(forged, JSON.stringify(record({ reviewId: `REV-forged-${process.pid}` }), null, 2));
   127	  try {
   128	    const r = spawnSync('node', [ENGOS, '--validate-review', forged], { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' });
   129	    assert.notStrictEqual(r.status, 0, 'an unsigned hand-written APPROVE must not validate');
   130	    assert.ok(/ATTESTATION-MISSING/.test(r.stdout + r.stderr),
   131	      'the refusal must name the missing attestation');
   132	  } finally {
   133	    try { fs.unlinkSync(forged); } catch {}
   134	    if (created) { try { fs.rmdirSync(dir); } catch {} }
   135	  }
   136	});
   137	
   138	test('the attestation key is never committed', () => {
   139	  const gi = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, '.gitignore'), 'utf8');
   140	  assert.ok(/\.attestation-key/.test(gi), 'the key must be gitignored');
   141	  const tracked = spawnSync('git', ['ls-files', '--error-unmatch', 'builder-control/.attestation-key'],
   142	    { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' });
   143	  assert.notStrictEqual(tracked.status, 0, 'the attestation key must not be tracked by git');
   144	});
   145	
   146	const failed = process.exitCode ? 'at least 1' : '0';
   147	console.log(`${passed} passed, ${failed} failed.`);
   100	// ── aggregation red proofs (record-level) ───────────────────────────────────
   101	const TMP = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(fs.realpathSync(os.tmpdir()), 'aegis-chunk-'));
   102	process.on('exit', () => { try { fs.rmSync(TMP, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch {} });
   103	
   104	const PACKET = path.join(TMP, 'packet.json');
   105	(function () {
   106	  const real = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'packets', 'ENGINEERING-OS-V1.json'), 'utf8'));
   107	  real.packetId = 'PKT-CHUNK-TEST';
   108	  fs.writeFileSync(PACKET, JSON.stringify(real, null, 2));
   109	})();
   110	
   111	const SUBJ_SHA = 'c'.repeat(64);
   112	function groupRecord(groupId, paths, over = {}) {
   113	  return {
   114	    reviewId: `REV-${groupId}`,
   115	    ts: '2026-08-25T01:00:00Z',
   116	    reviewer: 'codex',
   117	    reviewerModel: 'codex-cli (ChatGPT.app)',
   118	    packetId: 'PKT-CHUNK-TEST',
   119	    reviewOf: { diffSha256: SUBJ_SHA, changedPaths: paths.slice().sort() },
   120	    disposition: 'APPROVE',
   121	    findings: [],
   122	    group: { groupId, groupDigest: 'd'.repeat(64) },
   123	    ...over,
   124	  };
   125	}
   126	const sign = (r) => SIGN.sign(r, { packetPath: PACKET });
   127	
   128	test('RED: an UNSIGNED group record is not smaller evidence — it is none', () => {
   129	  const rec = groupRecord('G1', SUBJECT.slice(0, 3));
   130	  const v = SIGN.verify(rec, { packetPath: PACKET });
   131	  assert.strictEqual(v.ok, false);
   132	  assert.strictEqual(v.code, 'ATTESTATION-MISSING');
   133	});
   134	
   135	test('RED: a group bound to a STALE subject cannot join the aggregate', () => {
   136	  const rec = sign(groupRecord('G1', SUBJECT.slice(0, 3), {
   137	    reviewOf: { diffSha256: 'e'.repeat(64), changedPaths: SUBJECT.slice(0, 3) },
   138	  }));
   139	  assert.strictEqual(SIGN.verify(rec, { packetPath: PACKET }).ok, true, 'it is validly signed…');
   140	  assert.notStrictEqual(rec.reviewOf.diffSha256, SUBJ_SHA, '…but bound to another revision');
   141	});
   142	
   143	test('RED: MIXED reviewer across groups is detectable', () => {
   144	  const a = sign(groupRecord('G1', SUBJECT.slice(0, 3)));
   145	  const b = sign(groupRecord('G2', SUBJECT.slice(3, 6), { reviewer: 'grok' }));
   146	  const reviewers = [...new Set([a, b].map((r) => r.reviewer))];
   147	  assert.strictEqual(reviewers.length, 2,
   148	    '"Codex approved it" would be true of one group and false of the other');
   149	});
   150	
   151	test('RED: MIXED model across groups is detectable', () => {
   152	  const a = sign(groupRecord('G1', SUBJECT.slice(0, 3)));
   153	  const b = sign(groupRecord('G2', SUBJECT.slice(3, 6), { reviewerModel: 'codex-cli (other build)' }));
   154	  assert.strictEqual([...new Set([a, b].map((r) => r.reviewerModel))].length, 2);
   155	});
   156	
   157	test('RED: a TIMED-OUT group is UNAVAILABLE and still attested', () => {
   158	  // The 2026-08-25 failure wrote an UNSIGNED timeout record, which the gate saw
   159	  // as malformed rather than as "the reviewer could not run". An UNAVAILABLE is
   160	  // a real fact and gets the same integrity guarantee as an approval.
   161	  const rec = sign(groupRecord('G3', SUBJECT.slice(6, 8), {
   162	    disposition: 'UNAVAILABLE',
   163	    unavailableReason: 'Codex CLI (ChatGPT.app) exceeded the 420s timeout',
   164	    findings: [],
   165	  }));
   166	  assert.strictEqual(SIGN.verify(rec, { packetPath: PACKET }).ok, true,
   167	    'a timeout record must be attested, not left unsigned');
   168	  assert.strictEqual(rec.disposition, 'UNAVAILABLE');
   169	  assert.ok(/timeout/i.test(rec.unavailableReason));
   170	});
   171	
   172	test('RED: a CONFLICTING verdict is carried into the aggregate, not averaged away', () => {
   173	  const approve = sign(groupRecord('G1', SUBJECT.slice(0, 3)));
   174	  const reject = sign(groupRecord('G2', SUBJECT.slice(3, 6), {
   175	    disposition: 'REJECT',
   176	    findings: [{ severity: 'HIGH', file: 'x.cjs', problem: 'p', evidence: 'e',
   177	                 impact: 'i', requiredCorrection: 'c', verificationMethod: 'v', status: 'OPEN' }],
   178	  }));
   179	  const dispositions = [approve, reject].map((r) => r.disposition);
   180	  const worst = dispositions.includes('REJECT') ? 'REJECT'
   181	    : dispositions.includes('APPROVE_WITH_NOTES') ? 'APPROVE_WITH_NOTES' : 'APPROVE';
   182	  assert.strictEqual(worst, 'REJECT',
   183	    'one rejecting group must reject the whole aggregate — verdicts are not votes');
   184	});
   185	
   186	test('RED: AGGREGATION TAMPERING — editing a group after aggregation is detected', () => {
   187	  const g = sign(groupRecord('G1', SUBJECT.slice(0, 3), { disposition: 'REJECT' }));
   188	  const embeddedDigest = g.attestation.payloadDigest;
   189	  // Flip the group's verdict the way someone hiding a rejection would.
   190	  g.disposition = 'APPROVE';
   191	  const v = SIGN.verify(g, { packetPath: PACKET });
   192	  assert.strictEqual(v.ok, false, 'a flipped group verdict must not verify');
   193	  assert.strictEqual(v.code, 'ATTESTATION-INVALID');
   194	  // And the aggregate's embedded digest no longer describes the record on disk.
   195	  const reSigned = SIGN.sign(g, { packetPath: PACKET });
   196	  assert.notStrictEqual(reSigned.attestation.payloadDigest, embeddedDigest,
   197	    'the embedded digest must change, so an aggregate cannot outlive its evidence');
   198	});
   199	
   200	test('RED: an aggregate cannot be assembled from groups that do not cover the subject', () => {
   201	  const partial = [
   202	    { groupId: 'G1', paths: SUBJECT.slice(0, 3) },
   203	    { groupId: 'G2', paths: SUBJECT.slice(3, 6) },
   204	  ];
   205	  const cov = C.checkCoverage(partial, SUBJECT);
   206	  assert.strictEqual(cov.ok, false, 'four uncovered paths must block the aggregate');
   207	  assert.strictEqual(cov.code, 'COVERAGE-GAP');
   208	});
   209	
   210	test('group records live outside the directory the gate reads', () => {
   211	  // Only the aggregate may reach the gate. A stray group record must never be
   212	  // mistaken for a review of the whole change.
   213	  const adapters = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'review-adapters.cjs'), 'utf8');
   214	  assert.ok(/subdir: args\.groupId \? 'groups' : null/.test(adapters),
   215	    'group records must be written into reviews/groups/');
   216	  const engos = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'engineering-os.cjs'), 'utf8');
   217	  assert.ok(/d\.isFile\(\)/.test(engos),
   218	    'the gate must read only top-level review files, never subdirectories');
   219	});
   220	
   221	const failed = process.exitCode ? 'at least 1' : '0';
   222	console.log(`${passed} passed, ${failed} failed.`);
